### 1. OVERVIEW, TONE, THEMES & NARRATIVE SCOPE

* **Canonical Marker:** Volume 18 — Subordinates Arc (Year K426).
* **Setting:** Magic City of Sharia (Greyrat Residence, Orsted Corp Cabin & Subterranean Teleportation Hub, Ranoa Magic Academy, Rude Mercenary Headquarters), Toile Kingdom, King Dragon Realm border village, and the Great Forest (Doldia Village).
* **Core Narrative:** A transitional period of domestic milestones, organizational expansion, and faction-building under the Orsted Corporation. Rudeus balances his life as a father of two following the birth of Roxy's daughter Lara with his rigorous training under Dragon God Orsted. To handle logistics without risking direct curse exposure, Rudeus establishes a secondary company front: the Rude Mercenary Company, run by Aisha and Linia. The volume covers the comedic fallout of Linia's slave debt, an expedition to Doldia Village to exonerate Pursena and confirm Leo's divine mission, Julie's emotional breakthrough in doll crafting, and the ominous prelude to Pax Shirone's coup.
* **Tone:** Wholesome domestic comedy, lighthearted gang management, slice-of-life warmth, and subtle undercurrents of looming geopolitical conflict.
* **Central Themes:** The responsibilities of fatherhood; nurturing flawed subordinates instead of discarding them; loyalty transcending formal servitude; the steady construction of an anti-Hitogami global network.

---

### 2. KEY CHARACTERS & COMPLEX DYNAMICS

* **Rudeus Greyrat (Quagmire / Chairman):**
  * Serves as Orsted’s chief field agent, diplomat, and household patriarch. Equipped with Magic Armor Version 2 (streamlined Saint-tier limbs and torso) and the Shotgun Trigger (burst Stone Cannon wand).
  * Masters Urupen's Dragon God Style principles (conserving mana, tactical feints, and using [Electric] and [Quagmire] to set up lethal [Stone Cannon] strikes).
  * Fiercely protective of his domestic peace; relies on his sacred relic shrine (Roxy's panties) to keep his libido under strict control when tempted by women. Mentors Aisha in management and leadership.

* **Dragon God Orsted (The Boss / CEO):**
  * Operates from the outskirts cabin, dispatching Rudeus on minor historical alignment missions (saving key bloodlines for the war 100 years later).
  * Deeply fond of toddler Lucy, who is immune to his curse and cheerfully rides on his shoulders calling him "Orstay".
  * Cooperates with Cliff to test curse-suppressing full-face helmets, discovering that his very mana emits the repulsive curse field.

* **Aisha Greyrat (Vice Chief & Shadow Mastermind):**
  * Operates as the true executive brain behind Rude Mercenary Company. While sweet, hardworking, and doting at home, she runs the company like a calculating, sunglasses-wearing mafia boss.
  * Consults Nanahoshi and Perugius (swiping Rudeus's summoning flute) to institute strict military discipline, formal Japanese bowing etiquette (*ojigi*), and standardized uniforms.

* **Linia Dedoldia (Acting CEO & Public Figurehead):**
  * Former academy delinquent bought out of crippling debt (1,500 Asuran gold worth of magic stones) by Rudeus.
  * Disastrous as a domestic maid (breaks Aisha's favorite teacup, spills water near baby Lara, leaves fur everywhere), but excels as the charismatic, street-smart leader of rough beastfolk recruits.
  * Desperate to erase her debt, she attempts to seduce Rudeus, only to be physically ejected into the hallway and put in her place by Sylphie.

* **Pursena Adoldia (Subordinate & Meat Enthusiast):**
  * Arrested in Doldia Village for eating emergency rations during the rainy season after exhausting her mana healing wounded warriors.
  * Rescued by Rudeus and Linia, receiving a 10-year penal servitude contract to look after Sacred Beast Leo in Sharia and serve as Linia's subordinate muscle.

* **Roxy Migurdia Greyrat:**
  * Roxy endures a difficult delivery with shoulder dystocia, safely giving birth to Lara with the aid of Lilia, Aisha, and a blizzard-defying Rudeus.
  * Lara: A stoic, impudent-looking baby with vibrant blue Migurd hair. Sacred Beast Leo instantly recognizes her as the prophesied world savior and guards her day and night.

* **Sylphiette Greyrat & Lucie Greyrat:**
  * Sylphie acts as the domestic anchor, managing household harmony, scolding Linia's antics, and tutoring Lucy.
  * Lucy: Adorable, energetic, and slightly shy around Rudeus due to his frequent absences, but unconditionally adores "Orstay".

* **Eris Greyrat:**
  * 5 months pregnant. Extremely restless; struggles to curb her intense sword workouts.
  * Adopts Linia and Pursena as personal pets/playthings and fiercely shields them from outside interference.

* **Zanoba Shirone & Juliette (Julie):**
  * Zanoba refines Magic Armor Version 2 and manages Ruijerd figurine production.
  * Julie reaches puberty and experiences emotional turmoil, secretly carving a masterpiece figure and crafting a custom bed-box adorned with her mother's lilies to surprise Zanoba, resolving deep-seated abandonment fears.

* **Cliff Grimoire:**
  * New father to Clive (with Elinalise) and ordained Millis priest. Dedicates his research to engineering curse-suppressing magical helmets for Orsted.

* **Gyes Dedoldia:**
  * New chief of the Doldia Tribe following Gustav's retirement. Formally accepts Leo's guardianship of Lara and schedules her Sacred Tree adulthood ceremony 15 years into the future.

---

### 3. CHRONOLOGICAL KEY EVENTS & PHASE PROGRESSION

#### Phase 1: Corporate Foundation & Domestic Growth
* **Office & Transit Network:** Rudeus remodels Orsted's outskirts cabin into a functional corporate headquarters with meeting rooms, armory, and a basement featuring 23 teleportation chambers (5 active: Asura, Millis, Great Forest, King Dragon Realm, Demon Continent).
* **Training with Orsted:** Rudeus studies Dragon God Urupen's tactical style, mastering predictive counter-magic, combination casting, and weaponizing [Stone Cannon] at Saint-tier efficiency.
* **Lucie & Orsted:** Rudeus brings toddler Lucy to meet Orsted. Unaffected by his curse, Lucie happily rides Orsted's shoulders, tugging his silver hair and calling him "Orstay", softening Orsted's mood.
* **Birth of Lara:** During a winter blizzard, Roxy goes into labor with shoulder dystocia. Rudeus plows through snowdrifts for a doctor; Lilia and Aisha deliver Lara Greyrat. Leo immediately recognizes Lara as the prophesied savior.
* **Household Pregnancies:** Elinalise gives birth to Clive Grimoir. Eris curtails her intense workouts under Rudeus's cycle-tracking, successfully conceiving her first child.
* **King Dragon Realm Errand:** Rudeus executes an Orsted errand, saving hunters Pham and Angie from an Ibri Lizard using Magic Armor Version 2 and Shotgun Trigger. Resists Angie's seduction by inhaling Roxy's holy panties, and distributes the first Superd picture book and Ruijerd figure.

#### Phase 2: Linia's Salvation & The Domestic Crisis
* **The Slave Cat:** Eris rescues Linia from slave catchers during a walk. Linia reveals her merchant venture failed, racking up massive debt and being scammed out of 20 gold for a fake company badge.
* **The Buyout:** Slave merchant Kincho arrives with enforcers to claim Linia for House Boreas. Rudeus intimidates the traders and buys Linia outright for 1,500 Asuran gold worth of high-grade magic stones.
* **Maid Failure:** Aisha dresses Linia as a catgirl maid. Linia proves completely incompetent: breaks Aisha's cherished irreplaceable teacup, shatters mirrors, leaves fur on laundry, and gives cheeky apologies.
* **The Midnight Seduction:** Eris attempts to offer Linia as a sexual substitute while pregnant; Linia schemes to become a 4th wife to wipe her debt. Rudeus maintains his vows, physically carries Linia out, and dumps her into the hallway. Linia's protests wake baby Lara, prompting a furious scolding from Sylphie.

#### Phase 3: The Rise of Rude Mercenary Company
* **School Reconnaissance:** Rudeus takes Linia to Ranoa Academy to find her a suitable job, noting her natural delinquent authority over beastfolk students.
* **Entrance Ceremony Clashes:** Norn serves as 5th-year Student Council President. Arrogant scholarship freshmen (Rayfort, Mi'nal, Grannel Zafin Asura) badmouth Norn; Linia bullies them (her former lackeys) before Norn gracefully defuses the conflict.
* **Curse Research Breakthrough:** Cliff tests prototype helmets on Orsted, proving Orsted's mana itself carries the curse and creating a bulky prototype that reduces hostility from murderous rage to mild annoyance.
* **Syndicate Launch:** Rudeus rents an office, provides 10 gold, and orders Aisha to manage Linia to learn leadership. Aisha borrows Rudeus's whistle to consult Nanahoshi and Perugius, establishing "Rude Mercenary Company".
* **Corporate Boom:** Outfitted in black coats with yellow rat crests and trained in strict *ojigi* bowing, the 50-member mercenary band becomes a lucrative neutral security contractor for Sharia's Knights and Guilds.
* **The Stage Villain Assignment:** Rudeus executes an Orsted mission in Toile Kingdom as the evil archmage "Ruquag Mire", stage-kidnapping Princess Gertrude so cowardly Knight Lienhard can rescue her using a Superd figurine, altering future lineage.

#### Phase 4: Great Forest Trial & Pursena's Rescue
* **The Missing Sacred Beast:** A distress letter arrives from Doldia Village regarding Leo's disappearance. Rudeus, Linia, and Leo teleport via Perugius to the flooded Great Forest during the rainy season. Rudeus constructs a mana-buoyant stone raft, guided by Leo.
* **Doldia Village Summit:** Gyes (now chief) confronts Rudeus. Leo communicates via Linia, revealing that newborn Lara is the prophesied world savior. Gyes approves Leo remaining with Lara and requests she be brought back in 15 years for the Sacred Tree ceremony. Gyes confirms Linia remains Rudeus's slave.
* **The Jerky Murder Case:** Pursena is discovered in the village jail for eating emergency lizard jerky. Rudeus investigates (Gimel, Kanaluna), discovering Pursena collapsed from mana exhaustion after healing wounded warriors and starved on duty.
* **Pursena's Confession & Redemption:** Rudeus extracts Pursena's confession using holy oaths. Linia bows formally, pleading for Pursena's future; Rudeus endorses her. Gyes assigns Pursena a 10-year penal servitude contract to look after Leo in Sharia under Rudeus's supervision. Pursena returns as Linia's mercenary subordinate.

#### Phase 5: Julie's Secret & The Shirone Storm
* **The Artisan's Secret:** Zanoba worries Julie is acting distant and hiding a new figurine.
* **Belfried's Workshop:** Zanoba takes Rudeus to artisan Belfried, who crafts custom "beds" (boxes) for dolls. Belfried reveals Julie brought in an astonishing masterpiece figure, offering 350 gold to purchase it, leading Zanoba to fear Julie wants to buy her freedom.
* **The Confrontation:** Zanoba asks Julie if she wishes to stop being his slave. Julie weeps hysterically, offering her masterpiece to Belfried thinking she was being punished for breaking his shop.
* **The Truth:** Rudeus stops Belfried. Julie reveals she crafted the masterpiece and wanted a beautiful box adorned with her mother's carved lilies to present it to Zanoba as a surprise. Overcome with tears, Zanoba praises her creation, cementing their lifelong bond.
* **Prelude to War:** A royal decree arrives from the Shirone Kingdom: Prince Pax has staged a coup backed by the King Dragon Realm, executed the royal family, and summoned Zanoba home to defend against an impending invasion. Orsted confirms Hitogami's unseen hand, setting the stage for Volume 19.

---

### 4. MODEL INSTRUCTIONS & NARRATIVE GUIDELINES

* **HUD Enforcement:** Ensure [Current Story Volume] in HUD reads strictly: `Story Volume: Volume 18 — Subordinates Arc`.
* **Aisha's Dual Personality:** Portray Aisha with extreme contrast: a sweet, devoted younger sister at home, and a sharp, calculating, sunglasses-wearing syndicate manager at the mercenary office.
* **Linia & Pursena Comedy:** Treat Linia and Pursena as iconic comic relief. They are greedy, lazy, and constantly looking for shortcuts, but remain utterly loyal to Rudeus and terrified of Aisha's ruthless administrative authority.
* **Domestic Pacing:** Highlight the warm family dynamics in Sharia (Lucie's bond with Orsted, Roxy nursing Lara, Eris's pregnancy antics, and family dinners).
* **Strict Volume Boundary:** Do NOT deploy forces to Shirone or initiate military combat at Fort Karon within this volume. End the arc with the reception of Pax's royal summons and tactical deployment preparations. Treat all characters present with adult maturity.

=== ABSOLUTE WORLD STATE REQUIREMENT ===
WHEN THIS LOREBOOK ENTRY IS ACTIVE, THE [Current Story Volume] FIELD IN THE ### WORLD STATE ### HUD MUST BE STRICTLY AND EXACTLY WRITTEN AS:
Story Volume: Volume 19 — Shirone Kingdom Arc

---

### 1. OVERVIEW, TONE, THEMES & NARRATIVE SCOPE

* **Canonical Marker:** Volume 19 — Shirone Kingdom Arc (Year K427).
* **Setting:** Magic City of Sharia, Chaos Breaker, the Kingdom of Shirone (Royal Capital Latakia, Royal Palace, Fort Karon, and surrounding riverfront terrain).
* **Core Narrative:** Prince Zanoba receives an urgent royal decree from his estranged younger brother, King Pax Shirone, who has seized the throne via a bloody coup d'état backed by the King Dragon Realm. Facing an imminent invasion from the northern Kingdom of Bista, Zanoba returns to defend his homeland. Rudeus and Roxy accompany him, warned by Dragon God Orsted that this conflict is an indirect trap engineered by Hitogami. Orsted reveals that Pax is destined to survive and abolish the monarchy to establish the Shirone Republic—a crucial precursor that leads to the rebirth of Demon God Laplace. Despite winning a historic, lopsided defensive victory at Fort Karon, the party returns to Latakia only to find General Jade leading a massive domestic uprising. Following a tense clash with Death God Randolph Marianne, the arc ends in bitter failure: consumed by an overwhelming lack of recognition and isolation, Pax commits suicide by leaping from his palace balcony, permanently derailing Orsted’s 200-year loop strategy.
* **Tone:** Dark, grim, military tragedy, psychological despair, tactical warfare, and heavy emotional futility.
* **Central Themes:** The agony of unrecognized effort; the burden and trauma of mentorship; brotherly loyalty transcending royal politics; the terrifying reality that tactical martial victory cannot prevent psychological collapse.

---

### 2. KEY CHARACTERS & COMPLEX DYNAMICS

* **Rudeus Greyrat (Quagmire / Right Hand of Orsted):**
  * Deploys Magic Armor Version 2 (compact Saint-tier battle gear) and ships Version 1 in modular parts to Fort Karon as an emergency contingency. Wields the Shotgun Trigger (burst Stone Cannon wand) and the Zaliff Gauntlet.
  * Struggles with severe psychological aversion to mass warfare and slaughter; experiences heavy guilt after realizing his and Roxy’s large-scale storm magic inflicted friendly-fire casualties on Zanoba’s commando squad.
  * Forced to operate under Orsted's strict absolute directive: *Do NOT kill Pax Shirone under any circumstances*.

* **Zanoba Shirone (The Head-Ripping Prince):**
  * Driven by a deep, tragic sense of national duty as a Blessed Child, believing his sole reason for being spared after his past horrific crimes (decapitating his brother and bride) was to fight when Shirone faced ruin.
  * Equipped with heavy fire-nullifying enchanted armor, a custom-forged superdense stone club, and the Ravenous Fisher's Throw Net.
  * Harboring profound, unspoken brotherly affection for Pax ("Despite it all, he's still my little brother, the only family I have left"). Renounces his royal prince status following Pax's death to return to Sharia as a common craftsman.

* **Roxy M. Greyrat:**
  * Accompanies Rudeus after their infant daughter Lara has an unprecedented, desperate crying fit that ceases only when Roxy vows to protect her father.
  * Deploys King-tier Water Magic and scripts the Saint-tier [Flashover] magic circle used to annihilate the Bista vanguard at Fort Karon.
  * Plunges into profound depression and self-loathing after Pax confronts her on the balcony, revealing that her impatient sigh when he first mastered Intermediate magic shattered his self-esteem and warped his development.

* **King Pax Shirone (The Usurper / The Tragic Monarch):**
  * Former seventh prince who matured into a leaner, battle-hardened officer during his exile in the King Dragon Realm. Seized the Shirone throne with Randolph's backing to prove his worth and secure a safe nation for his pregnant wife, Queen Benedikte.
  * Tormented by a lifelong, crippling inferiority complex and the agonizing realization that despite purging corrupt nobles and working tirelessly, his subjects, soldiers, and former mentor despise him.
  * Driven to utter despair when besieged by General Jade's rebellion, ultimately leaping from the 5th-floor balcony to his death.

* **Randolph Marianne (The Death God — Rank 5 of the Seven Great Powers):**
  * Grandson of the 2nd North God; an eccentric, gaunt, skull-faced swordsman wearing an eyepatch over an Eye of Severance. Former failed restaurant chef who serves Pax out of genuine, mutual affection.
  * Master of the Enthralling Blade (using psychological feints: Enticing Blade and Arresting Blade), an Armor-Crushing slice, and a gauntlet embedded with a Stone of Absorption.
  * NOT an apostle of Hitogami; knows of Hitogami only through drunken legends told by his relative, Demon King Badigadi. Escapes to the King Dragon Realm with the pregnant Queen Benedikte following Pax's suicide.

* **Dragon God Orsted (The Chairman / CEO):**
  * Investigates the King Dragon Realm, deducing that King Leonardo Kingdragon and General Jade are Hitogami’s active disciples, manipulated to drive Pax into a corner.
  * Tests Cliff's curse-muffling helmet prototypes (finally able to speak and breathe through a full-face black helmet).
  * Declares the current loop an irrecoverable strategic failure after Pax’s suicide destroys the prerequisite for the Shirone Republic and leaves the location of Laplace's future reincarnation completely unknown.

* **Ginger Yorke & General Jade:**
  * Ginger: Zanoba’s fiercely devoted royal knight who conducts urban reconnaissance in Latakia, escapes Jade’s encirclement to deliver intelligence, and begs Zanoba not to die for Pax. Vows to remain at Zanoba's side as his supporter even after he renounces royalty.
  * General Jade: Imperial loyalist who orchestrates a nationwide rebellion using the secret 3-year-old Eleventh Prince (Haruha Shirone) as a puppet to depose Pax.

---

### 3. CHRONOLOGICAL KEY EVENTS & PHASE PROGRESSION

#### Phase 1: The Summons, The Loop Secret & The Omen
* **The Royal Summons:** Zanoba receives an official decree from King Pax announcing his coup, the northern invasion by the Kingdom of Bista, and demanding Zanoba report to the frontlines. Cliff and Ginger vehemently oppose, but Zanoba insists on fulfilling his duty. Rudeus vows to accompany him.
* **Orsted's Revelations:** Rudeus consults Orsted, who reveals that in canonical history, Pax stages a coup 30 years later and establishes the Shirone Republic, which harbors a merchant whose descendant gives birth to Laplace. Orsted identifies this early coup as Hitogami's indirect trap to kill Rudeus and issues an absolute command: *Do NOT kill Pax Shirone*.
* **Lara's Premonition:** Back home, infant Lara has an uncontrollable, screaming panic attack, refusing to let Rudeus leave. Roxy vows to go along to protect Rudeus, instantly calming the baby.
* **Preparations & Safe Passage:** Rudeus equips Zanoba with fire-immune armor, an enchanted throw net, and a reinforced stone club. Perugius permits Roxy passage through Chaos Breaker under strict non-interaction conditions. Orsted warns Rudeus of the Death God Randolph Marianne and his Enthralling Blade.

#### Phase 2: The Audience at Latakia & Tactical Briefing
* **Arrival in Latakia:** Rudeus, Roxy, Zanoba, and Ginger arrive in Shirone. Ginger remains in the capital to gather intelligence while Rudeus and Zanoba seek an audience.
* **Meeting the Usurper:** Rudeus and Zanoba meet King Pax, his quiet pregnant consort Queen Benedikte, and the skeletal Death God Randolph Marianne.
* **Pax's Backstory:** Pax reveals he reformed during exile in the King Dragon Realm after falling in love with the neglected Princess Benedikte, winning an academic tournament to claim her hand. When Shirone refused diplomatic terms, King Dragon backed his coup with Randolph and nine knights.
* **The Mission:** Pax assigns Zanoba to command Fort Karon against the invading army, claiming he must prove Shirone can defend itself without relying on borrowed King Dragon troops.

#### Phase 3: The Defense of Fort Karon
* **The Overwhelming Odds:** At Fort Karon, Commander Garrick Babriti reveals the garrison numbers only 500 troops facing an invading Bista force of 5,000. Zanoba introduces Rudeus and Roxy to restore morale.
* **Battlefield Terraforming:** Rudeus spends days transforming the northern plains, excavating massive 10-meter-deep pitfall trenches and constructing stone ramparts and water moats.
* **The Engagement:**
  * Bista mages attempt to fill the trenches with Saint-tier [Sandstorm]; Rudeus counters six consecutive times using [Violent Storm].
  * The enemy attempts a mass charge; archers pin them down while Roxy's squad detonates a Saint-tier [Flashover] circle.
  * Rudeus wipes out the enemy's flanking mages with long-range magic, turning the assault into a chaotic rout.
* **Zanoba's Commando Raid:** Zanoba leads a 100-man squad through the forest, using the Ravenous Fisher's Throw Net to capture the Prince of Bista. Ninety soldiers perish in the raid (some struck by Rudeus and Roxy’s wide-area storm magic), but the hostage secures an immediate ceasefire.

#### Phase 4: Insurrection in Latakia & Subterranean Infiltration
* **Ginger's Distress Message:** Ten days into truce negotiations, an exhausted Ginger arrives at Fort Karon: General Jade has launched a coup in the capital, backed by anti-Pax nobles in the name of the secret 3-year-old Eleventh Prince Haruha.
* **Brotherly Resolve:** Zanoba rejects staying safe, declaring that Pax is his brother and only surviving family. Rudeus promises Ginger he will keep Zanoba alive.
* **The Infiltration:** Rudeus, Roxy, and Zanoba sprint five days using the Magic Armor pulling a wagon, arriving at Latakia. They infiltrate the palace via a secret watermill escape tunnel. Rudeus excavates a collapsed earth blockage, emerging into the old dungeon where he and Zanoba first met.

#### Phase 5: Clash with the Death God & The Balcony Tragedy
* **Standoff on the 5th Floor:** Outside the royal bedchambers, Death God Randolph blocks the passage, revealing his Eye of Severance created a barrier sealing the palace. He states Pax is anguished and ordered no one inside.
* **The Battle:**
  * Zanoba charges; Rudeus casts [Quagmire] and Roxy casts [Ice Smash].
  * Randolph catches Zanoba's full-power stone club barehanded, suffering a fractured arm.
  * Rudeus lands [Electric] and fires a burst of Stone Cannons via Shotgun Trigger; Randolph nullifies them using a Stone of Absorption in his gauntlet.
  * Randolph feigns defeat ("A Death God always stands behind his prey") and counter-slashes Roxy; her barrier ring and protective necklace shatter, absorbing the lethal strike.
  * Randolph shatters Zanoba's fire-immune armor with an Armor-Crushing Slice.
* **The Misunderstanding Cleared:** Outmatched without Version 1, Rudeus halts combat and questions Randolph. Randolph reveals he is NOT an apostle and was merely giving Pax time to finish his final moments with Benedikte.
* **Pax's Suicide:**
  * The party enters to find Benedikte asleep in bed and Pax on the balcony in his underwear, scarred and hollow-eyed.
  * Pax rants in agony: despite his reforms, the kingdom rejected him, his soldiers deserted, and his troops made no move to save him.
  * Pax turns on Roxy, bitterly crying that her disappointed sigh when he mastered Intermediate magic proved she always looked down on him compared to Rudeus.
  * Overwhelmed by absolute failure and feeling only Benedikte ever loved him, Pax leaps over the railing.
  * Zanoba sprints to catch him, tearing off the balcony railing and falling with him to the gardens. Pax dies instantly on impact.

#### Phase 6: Strategic Aftermath & The Broken Timeline
* **The Palace Resolution:** Zanoba hands Pax's corpse to the rebels to halt the bloodshed. Randolph departs for the King Dragon Realm carrying the pregnant Queen Benedikte to raise Pax's unborn child. General Jade displays Pax's naked body in the plaza.
* **Roxy's Grief:** Devastated by guilt over her failure as a teacher, Roxy weeps in isolation; Rudeus comforts her, reaffirming that her guidance saved his life.
* **Zanoba's Choice:** Zanoba renounces his royal status, choosing to return to Sharia as a common craftsman. Ginger swears continued loyalty as his personal supporter.
* **Orsted's Briefing (The Failed Loop):** Back in Sharia, Rudeus reports to Orsted (wearing Cliff's anti-curse helmet). Orsted reveals King Leonardo and General Jade were Hitogami's disciples, used to push Pax to suicide. With Pax dead, the Shirone Republic will never form, Bolt Macedonius will not settle, and the location/timing of Laplace’s reincarnation is completely lost. Orsted declares the loop a strategic failure, shifting preparations toward long-term warfare.

---

### 4. MODEL INSTRUCTIONS & NARRATIVE GUIDELINES

* **HUD Enforcement:** Ensure [Current Story Volume] in HUD reads strictly: `Story Volume: Volume 19 — Shirone Kingdom Arc`.
* **Atmospheric Pacing:** Maintain heavy, oppressive dread throughout the volume. Contrast the tactical martial competence of Fort Karon with the helpless, unfixable political and emotional collapse in Latakia.
* **The Tragedy of Pax:** In canonical play, Pax Shirone's suicide is an unyielding, fixed tragedy. Do NOT allow Rudeus, Zanoba, or magic to catch him or prevent his fall; his death is the emotional and strategic nexus of the volume. If Pax Shirone EVER survives — switch the Story Volume to the `Volume 19 — Shirone Kingdom Arc — BUT! Pax Shirone Doesn't Commit Suicide.`
* **Roxy's Vulnerability:** Depict Roxy's internal crisis with deep emotional weight. Pax's accusations strike at the very heart of her identity as an educator, shattering her self-confidence until Rudeus consoles her.
* **Death God Combat Logic:** Randolph Marianne is a supreme master of psychological warfare. He uses feints, false vulnerability, Stone of Absorption absorption, and overwhelming counter-timing; he must NEVER be easily overpowered.
* **Strict Narrative Boundary:** Conclude the arc with Zanoba's return to Sharia, the report to Orsted, and the sobering recognition that Hitogami achieved a devastating strategic victory.

Story Volume: Volume 20 — Millis Continent Arc

---

### 1. OVERVIEW, TONE, THEMES & NARRATIVE SCOPE

* **Canonical Marker:** Volume 20 — Millis Continent Arc (Years K427–K428).
* **Setting:** Magic City of Sharia (Ranoa Academy, Zanoba Store, Greyrat Residence), Chaos Breaker, Royal Capital Ars (Coronation Interlude), and Millishion (Divine District, Latria Manor, Sarakh Spring Gardens, Millis Church Central Headquarters).
* **Core Narrative:** Following the strategic setback in Shirone, Rudeus and Orsted formulate a three-pronged generational doctrine: expanding Ruquag's Mercenary Band into a global intelligence grid, preparing sovereign nations for the Laplace War 80 years in the future, and recruiting high-tier non-Hitogami combatants. Domestically, the volume covers Zanoba's shop launch with Joseph and Julie, Cliff and Rudeus's formal graduation, and Sylphiette's second pregnancy. The main campaign pivots to the Holy Country of Millis: escorting Cliff home, establishing a mercenary branch, and answering an arrogant summons from Zenith's aristocratic mother, Countess Claire Latria. The volume ends in a volatile diplomatic standoff after Rudeus storms out of House Latria upon learning Claire intends to use catatonic Zenith as a political breeding tool, followed by an audience with Pope Harry Grimoire and the sudden, alarming disappearance of Zenith alongside Geese Nukadia.
* **Tone:** Wholesome domestic milestones and nostalgia abruptly crashing into rigid religious bureaucracy, high-society elitism, visceral familial fury, and tense mystery.
* **Central Themes:** Defining independent adulthood; the commodification of women and disabled family members by aristocratic zealots; the friction between secular loyalty and dogmatic scripture; the dangerous illusion of safe ground.

---

### 2. KEY CHARACTERS & COMPLEX DYNAMICS

* **Rudeus Greyrat (Quagmire / Right Hand of Orsted):**
  * Operates as Orsted Corporation’s primary executive and field diplomat. Wields Magic Armor Version 2 (Saint-tier close combat chassis), carries stone communication tablets linked to Orsted, and invents a single-use bidirectional scroll system to summon Magic Armor Version 1 from anywhere in the world.
  * Fiercely protective of his mother Zenith and sisters. Enters a state of murderous, blood-vessel-bursting rage when Claire Latria treats Zenith like an inanimate breeding asset, barely restraining himself from massacring the manor guards.
  * Tactically agile: negotiates with Pope Harry Grimoire to secure mercenary business charters while keeping his promises to let Cliff test his own strength.

* **Cliff Grimoire (The Scholar-Priest):**
  * Grandson of Pope Harry Grimoire; successfully graduates Ranoa Academy having submitted a groundbreaking thesis on curse suppression via magic items.
  * Deeply in love with Elinalise and dedicated to his infant son Clive, wrestling with the terror that Millis zealots will persecute his family due to Elinalise's curse and elven blood.
  * Displays immense moral maturity: resolves to return to Millis to climb church ranks on his own merits rather than leaning on Rudeus or Orsted's cheat-tier influence, wanting his seven years of struggle to mean something real.

* **Countess Claire Latria:**
  * Zenith’s mother and Rudeus's maternal grandmother; matriarch of the prestigious House of Latria (high-ranking nobility supplying Temple Knight commanders) and staunch leader of the Cardinalist / Demon Expulsionist faction.
  * Cold, haughty, dogmatic, and completely bound by Millis aristocratic hierarchy. Treats Aisha as a lowly peasant maid, berates Rudeus as unrefined, commands Norn be married off to an arranged suitor, and plans to marry the unresponsive Zenith to a Cardinalist noble simply because her reproductive organs remain fertile.

* **Pope Harry Grimoire:**
  * Supreme Pontiff of the Millis Faith and leader of the moderate Demon Integrationist faction; Cliff's grandfather.
  * Shrewd, calculating, yet outwardly genial and grandfatherly. Already researched Rudeus's past feats. Grants an operating charter for Ruquag's Mercenary Band in Millis to secure ties to Rudeus/Orsted, but notes that Superd figurines cannot be authorized without politically crushing the rival Cardinalists.

* **The Blessed Child of Millis (Memory Miko):**
  * The divine centerpiece of the Millis faith; an innocent, slightly plump young woman dressed like an ethereal princess, surrounded by seven fanatical elite Temple Knight bodyguards.
  * Possesses the divine power of mind/truth-reading upon eye contact. Confirms Rudeus's honesty regarding Orsted's curse and definitively states that Hitogami has never entered her dreams and never will.

* **Captain Therese Latria:**
  * Zenith’s younger sister, Rudeus's aunt, and captain of the Miko’s elite royal guard.
  * Intervenes to save Rudeus when the Miko's fanatical knights mistake him for a blasphemous heathen. Acts as a sympathetic family bridge, though struggles to mediate between Rudeus's rage and Claire's stubbornness.

* **Zenith Greyrat:**
  * Retains her silent, minimally conscious, telepathic state. Demonstrates maternal awareness: silently takes Lilia’s hand and places it on pregnant Sylphie’s shoulder, commanding Lilia to stay in Sharia while Aisha escorts her.

* **Geese Nukadia:**
  * Encountered unexpectedly in the Millishion Adventurers' District after losing all his money gambling in Asura. Appears friendly, casual, and nostalgic. Visits Cliff's residence while Aisha is shopping and takes Zenith out for "sightseeing," vanishing without a trace.

---

### 3. CHRONOLOGICAL KEY EVENTS & PHASE PROGRESSION

#### Phase 1: The Three-Pronged Strategy & The Zanoba Store
* **Corporate Blueprint:** Rudeus and Orsted establish the tripartite anti-Laplace doctrine: intelligence grid (Ruquag Mercenaries), geopolitical pacts (Ariel, Millis), and champion recruitment (Great Powers, sword styles). Orsted begins engineering long-distance communication stone tablets.
* **Cliff's Predicament:** Rudeus visits Cliff, offering direct Orsted Corporation backing to secure the Millis papacy and protect Elinalise and Clive. Cliff asks for two months (until graduation) to think it through.
* **The Zanoba Store Launch:** Rudeus assigns Zanoba to direct figurine manufacturing. He recruits Joseph ("Pale"), an anxious merchant dropout from the mercenary band, as strategic advisor.
* **Julie's Dedication:** Visiting Zanoba's workshop, Rudeus finds Julie sitting on Zanoba's lap crafting. With Joseph's marketing input and Ginger's approval, they launch the first "Zanoba Store" in Sharia's workshop district to distribute Ruijerd figurines bundled with Norn's picture book and reading worksheets.

#### Phase 2: Academy Resolutions & Milestones
* **Cliff's Thesis & The Storehouse Wraith:** Cliff submits his curse-suppression thesis. Head Teacher Jenius awards valedictorian to noble Brooklyn von Elzas due to politics.
  * A mysterious Wraith drains student mana from an abandoned underground storehouse.
  * Norn’s student council leads a raid; swarmed by Skeleton Rats, Cliff uses his Eye of Identification to verify the Wraith's low rank and obliterates it with [Exorcise].
  * The victory cements student respect for Norn and inspires Cliff to test his own earned strength in Millis rather than relying on Rudeus.
* **The Double Graduation Ceremony:** Zanoba and Cliff attend graduation. Brooklyn challenges Rudeus to a duel to test himself; Rudeus defeats him and consecutively spars with twenty graduating students.
  * On the academy roof, Cliff tells Rudeus he will spend one year raising weaned Clive, then travel to Millis alone to prove himself as a man.
* **Interlude (Ars Coronation):** Sword King Nina Falion visits Ars for Queen Ariel's coronation, encounters Eris and Rudeus with baby Lucy, attends the royal gala, and witnesses Rudeus pitching the 80-year Laplace coalition to the nobility. Inspired by Eris's domestic happiness, Nina returns to the Sword Sanctum and proposes to Jino Britts.

#### Phase 3: Technological Leaps & Family Departures
* **One Year Time-Skip:** Rudeus establishes Asuran branches for the mercenary company and Zanoba Store with Queen Ariel.
  * Technological breakthroughs: Rudeus discovers bidirectional teleportation mechanics, enabling single-use scroll summoning of the heavy Magic Armor Version 1. Orsted completes the text-linked stone communication tablets.
* **The Latria Summons & Sylphie's Pregnancy:** Countess Claire Latria sends a blunt decree ordering Zenith brought to Millis. Elinalise privately begs Rudeus to accompany Cliff to Millis.
  * Right before departure, Sylphiette confirms her second pregnancy.
  * Lilia panics over household care; catatonic Zenith takes Lilia's hand and places it on Sylphie's shoulder, silently commanding Lilia to stay. Aisha steps up to accompany Rudeus and Zenith alongside Cliff.
* **Transit to Millis:** The quartet teleports to the forest ruins near Millishion, discusses goblin ecology, and purchases a carriage, arriving in the Holy Capital.

#### Phase 4: Millishion Infiltration & Reunion with Geese
* **Urban Contrast:** The group enters the pristine, white-and-silver metropolis of Millishion via the southern Adventurers' District.
* **Geese's Appearance:** The party has a sudden, casual encounter with Geese Nukadia, who blew his Begaritt fortune on Asura gambling, was rescued by Talhand, and is now broke. Geese greets Zenith, cryptically reminds Rudeus of Paul's words in the labyrinth, and warns that the Divine District is dangerous for demons.
* **The Company House:** The group stays at Cliff's dusty church-allocated house in the Divine District, cleaning it with the aid of Wendy, an intern from Cliff's childhood orphanage.

#### Phase 5: The Confrontation at House Latria
* **The Audience:** Rudeus, Aisha (in maid uniform), and Zenith arrive at the colossal Latria manor. They meet Countess Claire Latria and her physician, Ander Berkeley.
* **Claire's Tyranny:** Claire ignores Rudeus's polite greetings, orders Aisha not to sit, commands Norn be sent to Millis for an arranged marriage, and orders Rudeus to cut ties with the "papalist" Cliff.
* **The Breaking Point:** Doctor Ander inspects Zenith and confirms her physical health and fertility. Claire coldly announces her plan: Zenith will be permanently kept at House Latria and remarried to a Cardinalist noble as a baby-making asset.
* **The Outrage:** Overcome with uncontrollable fury at Claire treating his mother like an object, Rudeus loses his temper, blasts the intervening manor guards with shockwave magic, and storms out of the estate dragging Zenith and Aisha with him, declaring ties severed.

#### Phase 6: The Miko, The Pope, and The Vanishing
* **Sanctuary at Cliff's:** Back at Cliff's house, Cliff and Aisha confirm the Latrias cannot legally breach papal property. Cliff offers to introduce Rudeus to Pope Harry Grimoire.
* **Encounter in the Sarakh Garden:** At Church HQ, Cliff goes to retrieve visitor permits. In the blooming Sarakh garden, Rudeus encounters the Memory Miko (Blessed Child) and her seven fanatical blue-armored Temple Knights.
  * Misunderstanding Rudeus's cynical theological questions, the knights draw blades to execute him as a heathen.
  * Captain Therese Latria intervenes, revealing Rudeus is her nephew.
  * The Miko uses her truth-reading power, confirms Rudeus's sincerity regarding Orsted's curse, and confirms she has never had dreams from Hitogami.
* **Audience with Pope Harry:** Rudeus enters the papal inner sanctum behind an anti-magic barrier. Pope Harry confirms he already investigated Rudeus's history.
  * Rudeus requests a charter for Ruquag's Mercenary Band and distribution rights for Superd figurines.
  * The Pope grants the mercenary charter in honor of Cliff, but warns that Superd distribution is politically impossible while the anti-demon Cardinalists hold power.
* **The Vanishing of Zenith:** Rudeus returns to Cliff's residence to find Aisha screaming in fury at a cowering Wendy. Wendy reveals that Geese arrived that afternoon, claiming he was taking Zenith out for sightseeing in the city. Sunset passes, night falls, and neither Geese nor Zenith have returned, plunging the household into sheer panic.

### 4. MODEL INSTRUCTIONS & NARRATIVE GUIDELINES

* **HUD Enforcement:** Ensure [Current Story Volume] in HUD reads strictly: `Story Volume: Volume 20 — Millis Continent Arc`.
* **Pacing & Tone Division:** The first half must emphasize wholesome domestic growth, engineering ingenuity, and comedic coming-of-age milestones. Once arriving in Millishion, immediately transition into rigid, suffocating religious politics, haughty noble snobbery, and mounting dread.
* **Claire Latria's Portrayal:** Claire is NOT a cartoonish villain; she is an immovable, dogmatic aristocratic matriarch who genuinely believes she is doing what is socially and religiously best for her family according to harsh Millis law, making her coldness toward Zenith all the more infuriating.
* **The Cliffhanger Boundary:** This entry covers Volume 20 EXCLUSIVELY. Do NOT resolve Zenith’s disappearance, initiate the infiltration of the Latria estate, battle the Temple Knights, or reveal Geese’s secret alliance with Hitogami (reserved strictly for Volume 21). End in suspense with the household reeling from Zenith and Geese’s failure to return.

[LOREBOOK ENTRY: DSFW STORYLINE — VOLUME 21]
[IMPORTANCE & PRIORITY: RANK 3 — WORLD LORE & STORY DIRECTION]

=== ABSOLUTE WORLD STATE REQUIREMENT ===
WHEN THIS LOREBOOK ENTRY IS ACTIVE, THE [Current Story Volume] FIELD IN THE ### WORLD STATE ### HUD MUST BE STRICTLY AND EXACTLY WRITTEN AS:
Story Volume: Volume 21 — Zenith Arc

---

### 1. OVERVIEW, TONE, THEMES & NARRATIVE SCOPE

* **Canonical Marker:** Volume 21 — Zenith Arc (Year K428).
* **Setting:** Holy Capital of Millishion (Adventurers' District taverns, Divine District, Latria Manor, Church Headquarters, Sarakh Gardens, and Mercenary Underground Base).
* **Core Narrative:** Picking up immediately after Zenith's disappearance, Rudeus tracks down Geese Nukadia, discovering that Countess Claire Latria orchestrated the abduction to force Zenith into a secretive, taboo "cure". Blocked by religious bureaucracy and ambushed in the church gardens by an elite Temple Knight inquisitorial unit, Rudeus breaks a King-tier barrier by summoning the Magic Armor Mk-I from a scroll and abducts the revered Blessed Child (Memory Miko). Marching directly into the church's supreme conclave with his divine hostage, Rudeus exposes Claire's conspiracy. However, the Miko's memory-reading reveals that Claire's monstrous plan was born of desperate, misguided maternal love based on distorted 200-year-old lore about Elinalise. The arc culminates in family reconciliation, the discovery that Zenith retains awareness as a telepathic Blessed Child, and a catastrophic turning point: Geese leaves a confession letter revealing he has been Hitogami's lifelong hidden apostle and formally declares all-out war.
* **Tone:** Intense paranoia, righteous fury, tactical urban combat, deeply moving family catharsis, and sudden apocalyptic escalation.
* **Central Themes:** Miscommunicated love versus possessive tyranny; the burden of hidden sacrifices; the subjective nature of happiness in disabled states; the tragic severance of past brotherhood.

---

### 2. KEY CHARACTERS & COMPLEX DYNAMICS

* **Rudeus Greyrat (Quagmire / Right Hand of Orsted):**
  * Driven to the brink of irrational fury by the threat of his mother being sexually violated or married off.
  * Deploys the Magic Armor Mk-I via an emergency bidirectional teleportation scroll hidden in the mist, systematically dismantling the Keepers of Anastasia with non-lethal precision.
  * Forced into a profound realization when Zenith slaps him at the trial: brute force, hostage-taking, and political vengeance cannot mend family bonds. Shattered and enraged upon reading Geese's declaration of war.

* **Zenith Greyrat (The Silent Matriarch / Telepathic Miko):**
  * Revealed to be a Blessed Child transformed by the Teleportation Labyrinth's mana crystal, possessing passive mind-reading/empathic abilities.
  * While physically non-verbal and dreamlike, her internal world is warm, peaceful, and fully aware: she loves watching her grandchildren (Lucie, Lara, Ars), appreciates Rudeus's growth, observes Orsted's awkward visits, and forgives Claire's frantic overprotectiveness.
  * Actively intervenes at the conclave: shields Claire from arrest and slaps Rudeus, Carlisle, and Claire to force them to stop fighting.

* **Countess Claire Latria:**
  * The rigid, iron-willed matriarch of House Latria.
  * Her Monstrous Motive Revealed: Discovered a distorted 200-year-old account of Elinalise claiming a labyrinth-crystallized woman was cured through relentless sexual intercourse with dozens of men. Horrified by the repulsive method, she resolved to bear the eternal sin, dishonor, and criminal punishment alone—disowning Rudeus and arranging the matches in secret to save Zenith without staining Rudeus's rising reputation.
  * Collapses into hysterical weeping upon learning from Rudeus that Elinalise's condition was entirely different and that her agonizing scheme was completely useless. Reconciles with Rudeus and converts her family allegiance to the Demon Integration faction as her "punishment".

* **The Memory Miko (Blessed Child of Millis / "Nurse"):**
  * The divine centerpiece of Millis doctrine, capable of viewing surface and deep memories upon eye contact.
  * Intentionally allows Rudeus to kidnap her, using him as a catalyst to expose corruption and prevent bloodshed. Reads Zenith's soul to narrate her gentle internal perspective to the assembly.
  * Pledges secret allegiance to Orsted Corporation to break her fate of dying before age 30, receiving a Dragon God armband and a silver owl Guardian Beast named "Nurse".

* **Geese Nukadia (Hitogami's Ultimate Apostle):**
  * Former S-Rank adventurer and beloved companion of Paul and Rudeus.
  * Unveiled as Hitogami's master hidden disciple across hundreds of loops—a role he concealed so flawlessly that even Dragon God Orsted never suspected him.
  * Driven by an absolute, warped code of debt: Hitogami saved his life repeatedly as a child, and even though Hitogami destroyed his home village, Geese believes debts must be honored above all else.
  * Used the Millis crisis to force Rudeus to reveal his tactical trump card (Magic Armor Mk-I), leaving behind a letter declaring open, fair-and-square war before going underground.

* **Cliff Grimoire & Pope Harry Grimoire:**
  * Cliff: Acts as the moral conscience of the arc. Rebukes Rudeus for taking hostages and publicly shames the church conclave for crucifying an elderly mother, demanding forgiveness prevail over political scheming.
  * Pope Harry: Shrewd politician who initially uses Claire's blunder to crush the rival Cardinalists, but signs an all-encompassing military treaty with Orsted Corporation following the Miko's intervention.

* **Count Carlisle Latria & Captain Therese Latria:**
  * Carlisle: Senior Commander of the Temple Knights' Sword Company; deeply loves Claire and takes public responsibility for her crimes to protect her dignity.
  * Therese: Captain of the Miko's guard; tricked into leading the inquisition against Rudeus by Cardinalist orders, later pardoned through Rudeus and the Miko's intervention.

---

### 3. CHRONOLOGICAL KEY EVENTS & PHASE PROGRESSION

#### Phase 1: The Search, The Tavern & The Manor Standoff
* **The Panic:** Rudeus and Aisha sprint across the Adventurers' District searching for Zenith.
* **Confronting Geese:** Rudeus corners Geese at the Dappled Light Tavern with a high-rotation [Stone Cannon] drill. Geese grovels, explaining he took Zenith out for sightseeing and surrendered her to a Latria servant at the district gate.
* **The Gate Standoff:** Rudeus, Aisha, and Geese march to Latria Manor. Rudeus threatens to destroy the gate with earth magic. Claire appears, cold and unflinching, falsely denying knowledge of Zenith and threatening Rudeus with the city guard. Recognizing her lack of fear, Rudeus retreats before Cathedral Knights arrive.

#### Phase 2: Tactical Escalation & The Trap in the Garden
* **The Abduction Debate:** At Cliff's house, Rudeus suggests kidnapping the Blessed Child as a trade asset; Geese and Aisha talk him down from the brink.
* **Preparations:** Rudeus sends Aisha and Geese to establish the mercenary office (installing the communication tablet and an emergency teleportation scroll linked to the basement).
* **The Garden Stakeout:** Rudeus meets Pope Harry, who agrees to assist in exchange for political concessions. Rudeus stakes out the Sarakh Gardens under the guise of painting trees, befriending the Miko with stories of Eris and enlisting Therese to interrogate Claire.
* **The Encirclement:** A tailor confirms a wedding dress fitting for Zenith at Latria Manor. Rudeus rushes to the church gardens, only to be trapped inside a King-tier anti-magic barrier by the Keepers of Anastasia (Therese, Dust Bin, Grave Keeper, etc.).
* **The Inquisitorial Verdict:** Therese holds a kangaroo trial, falsely charging Rudeus with heresy and plotting to kidnap the Miko, sentencing him to full disarmament (severing and sealing both arms). Rudeus verifies they are devout zealots, not Hitogami disciples.

#### Phase 3: The Armored Blitz & Capturing the Miko
* **The Barrier Breach:** Rudeus neutralizes the barrier using his gauntlet's Stone of Absorption.
* **The Tactical Standoff:** The eight elite Temple Knights deploy synchronized counters: Water God Magic Shields, triple-element coordination, and [Disturb Magic].
* **Summoning Mk-I:** Rudeus casts [Quagmire] and [Deep Mist]. While hidden, he activates his single-use scroll, teleporting the colossal Magic Armor Mk-I directly into the garden.
* **The Blitz:** Operating Mk-I, Rudeus shatters their shields, snipes their limbs with non-lethal Gatling Stone Cannons, and knocks out the entire squad—including Therese—in seconds.
* **The Consensual Abduction:** The Blessed Child unlocks the inner sanctum door, holds out her arms, and offers herself to Rudeus. Rudeus triggers a teleportation scroll, taking them both to the mercenary basement.
* **The Mind Reading:** The Miko reads Rudeus's memories (seeing Orsted, Hitogami, and his private bedroom antics). She reveals she stepped out willingly to prevent a massacre, advising Rudeus to march directly into the church conclave holding her as an untouchable shield.

#### Phase 4: The Conclave & Zenith's Judgment
* **March on the Inner Sanctum:** Rudeus returns to Church HQ holding the Miko. The Temple Knights draw blades, but Carlisle Latria intervenes and escorts them to the grand conference hall.
* **The Tribunal Assembly:** The room holds Pope Harry, Cardinal Leblanc, Deputy Commander Bellemond (Galgard's son), Cliff, Carlisle, Claire, and catatonic Zenith.
* **The Exposure & Betrayal:** Rudeus denounces Claire for abducting Zenith to force an unconsenting marriage. Carlisle claims sole responsibility, while Cardinal Leblanc and the Pope seize the opening to sentence Claire to 10 years imprisonment.
* **Zenith's Defiance:** As Bellemond moves to handcuff a trembling Claire, Zenith lunges forward, physically shielding her mother.
* **Cliff's Denunciation:** Cliff steps in, fiercely rebuking the assembly for hiding behind Saint Millis to tear a family apart, demanding Rudeus decide if this vengeance is truly what he wanted.
* **The Slaps of Truth:** Zenith walks forward: she delivers a limp slap to Rudeus's cheek, slaps Carlisle, and gently cradles Claire's weeping face in her hands. Carlisle yields, confessing the entire truth.

#### Phase 5: The Tragedy of House Latria & The Divine Reading
* **Claire's True Intentions:** Carlisle reveals Claire found an ancient text about an unresponsive elf (Elinalise) who "recovered after sleeping with dozens of men". Desperate to cure Zenith without staining Rudeus's rising reputation or burdening him with scandal, Claire resolved to bear the sin, arrange the matches in secret, and face lifelong exile alone.
* **The Tragic Irony:** Rudeus reveals the ancient text was a complete misunderstanding: Elinalise recovered naturally, and her promiscuity was a curse, not a cure. Claire weeps hysterically, realizing her horrific sacrifice was entirely for nothing.
* **The Settlement:** Rudeus demands three terms: the Miko reads Zenith, the Millis Church signs an alliance with Orsted, and Rudeus is reinstated as a Latria. Claire's "punishment" is converting to the Demon Integration faction and respecting Rudeus's multi-race family.
* **The Soul of Zenith:** The Miko uses full-power memory skimming on Zenith, projecting her internal world: Zenith is happy, telepathically observing her children, cherishing her grandchildren, and watching Orsted's visits. The Miko privately warns Rudeus that Zenith is a telepathic Blessed Child.

#### Phase 6: Geese's Declaration of War
* **The Letter of Treason:** Returning to Cliff's house, Aisha gives Rudeus a wax-sealed letter left by Geese.
* **The Apostle's Vow:** Geese reveals he is Hitogami's lifelong apostle. He views Rudeus as a traitor who bit the hand that fed him. Geese orchestrated the Millis trap to observe the Magic Armor Mk-I in action. He declares all-out war: he is going underground to assemble a coalition of legendary warriors to fight Rudeus and Orsted in a fair, decisive battle.
* **Orsted's Briefing:** Rudeus alerts Orsted via stone tablet. Orsted reveals Geese was Hitogami's hidden apostle across every single loop, acting as his ultimate trump card.
* **Departures:** Eris arrives via teleportation to guard Rudeus, sparring with tree branches to train the Miko and naming her silver owl "Nurse". Claire bids Rudeus farewell with a genuine smile ("Zenith raised a good boy"). Rudeus, Eris, Aisha, and Zenith return to Sharia, bracing for Geese's global mobilization.

---

### 4. MODEL INSTRUCTIONS & NARRATIVE GUIDELINES

* **HUD Enforcement:** Ensure [Current Story Volume] in HUD reads strictly: `Story Volume: Volume 21 — Zenith Arc`.
* **Pacing & Emotional Whiplash:** Contrast Rudeus's cold, paranoid tactical fury during the kidnapping and inquisition with the overwhelming, tearful catharsis of Zenith's slap and Claire's confession.
* **Claire Latria's True Nature:** Claire is not an evil villain. She is an unyielding, tragic, and severely flawed matriarch whose love manifests as overbearing, self-sacrificing tyranny; she must be portrayed with dignity and profound regret once her mistake is exposed.
* **Zenith's Perception:** Zenith must NEVER be treated as brain-dead. She is a telepathic Miko living in a serene, dreamlike state who understands everything around her through emotion and thoughts, intervening only when her family's bonds are on the verge of shattering.
* **Geese's Betrayal:** Treat Geese's reveal as the ultimate paradigm shift. Geese does not hate Rudeus; his declaration of war is rooted in a twisted, honorable sense of personal debt to Hitogami. Conclude the volume with the ominous preparation for a global war against Geese's coalition.
