Story Volume: Redundancy Volume 1 — New Beginnings Arc

---

### 1. OVERVIEW, TONE, THEMES & NARRATIVE SCOPE

* **Canonical Marker:** Redundancy Volume 1 — New Beginnings & Courtship Arc (Years K430–K432).
* **Setting:** Magic City of Sharia (Greyrat Residence, Ranoa Magic Academy, Orsted Corp Office), Biheiril Kingdom (Superd Village), and the Asura Kingdom (Royal Capital Ars, Silver Palace).
* **Core Narrative:** In the immediate peacetime following the Battle of Biheiril, everyday life resumes. Orsted reveals that in prior loops, Norn was fated to marry Ruijerd and bear a daughter who delivered the final blow to Laplace. Rudeus investigates Norn's feelings, learns her admiration has blossomed into mutual love, and orchestrates a heartwarming hybrid Superd/Millis wedding in Biheiril, resulting in the birth of Luicelia Superdia. Meanwhile, Lucy enters Ranoa Magic Academy; a paranoid Rudeus disguises himself in Orsted’s coat and helmet to spy on her classes, learning to trust her independence after Sylphiette scolds him. In Asura, Water Emperor Isolte Cruel seeks a husband who will surrender his surname per ancient Water God tradition; after rejecting dozens of perverted high-society nobles, the simple, honest gatekeeper Doga proposes with flowers and resigns his knighthood to protect her, winning her heart.
* **Tone:** Wholesome, domestic comedy, romantic warmth, sweet nostalgia, and tender parenting milestones.
* **Central Themes:** Courtship transcending social rank and race; paternal overprotection versus child autonomy; the quiet bravery of ordinary life.

---

### 2. KEY CHARACTERS & COMPLEX DYNAMICS

* **Rudeus Greyrat:**
  * Fully settled into domestic fatherhood, struggling with comedic helicopter-parent tendencies.
  * Fiercely protective of Norn's chastity, demanding her suitor have the "guts" to endure a punch from Eris before learning Ruijerd is her chosen partner.
  * Cosplays as "Fake Orsted" (wearing Orsted's spare anti-curse helmet and white dragon coat) to secretly monitor Lucy's first day at school, getting caught and put into seiza by Sylphiette.

* **Norn Greyrat & Ruijerd Superdia:**
  * Norn: Has matured from a sullen, insecure girl into a diligent guild worker and capable homemaker. Confesses her childhood admiration for Ruijerd deepened into profound romantic love during the Biheiril plague crisis.
  * Ruijerd: The honorable, 500-year-old warrior admits he fell in love with Norn while she nursed him. Readily duels Rudeus in the woods to prove his "guts" and vows to protect Norn for life.
  * Luicelia Superdia: Their firstborn daughter, born with green hair, a Superd tail, and a forehead gem.

* **Lucy Greyrat:**
  * Rudeus and Sylphiette's eldest daughter. Eager, brave, and deeply devoted to earning her legendary father's praise.
  * Challenges her academy professor on day one regarding mana pool capacity expansion, impressing her peers and making friends independently.

* **Sylphiette Greyrat:**
  * The firm, loving pillar of household discipline. Insists Lucy must walk to school alone to develop self-reliance, and catches Rudeus spying through a classroom window.

* **Doga & Isolte Cruel:**
  * Isolte: Water Emperor (succeeding the title of Water God Reida). Bound by the ancient tradition that her groom must abandon his surname and noble house, she rejects 21+ arrogant, perverted suitors.
  * Doga: The North Emperor gatekeeper of Ariel's private chamber. Simple-minded, profoundly honest, and hopelessly in love with Isolte since she bested him in sparring.
  * The Dynamic: Advised by his brother-in-law Hans, Doga buys a giant blue bouquet, confesses his love in front of Isolte's dojo, and renounces his royal knighthood to protect her as a househusband. Isolte is deeply moved by his pure, unselfish devotion and accepts his proposal.

* **Dragon God Orsted ("President"):**
  * Operates with quiet, paternal affection behind his intimidating facade. Confesses he has grown attached to Norn's child across multiple loops and actively loans Rudeus his helmet and coat.

---

### 3. CHRONOLOGICAL KEY EVENTS & PHASE PROGRESSION

#### Phase 1: Norn's Marriage & The Superd Union
* **Orsted's Directives:** Orsted catches Rudeus off guard by stating Norn should marry, revealing her fated partner across loops is Ruijerd Superdia and their child is destined to strike Laplace's weak point.
* **The Living Room Talk:** Rudeus cautiously questions Norn about her love life. Norn blushes, revealing she loves an older, reliable man (Ruijerd). When Rudeus mentions Ruijerd's name, Norn excitedly demands he proceed with the engagement.
* **The Duel of Guts:** Rudeus visits the Superd Village in Biheiril. In the forest, Ruijerd knocks Rudeus down with a spear sweep to prove his "guts," confessing he fell in love with Norn while she cared for him during the plague.
* **Sisters' Midnight Talk:** In Sharia, Aisha visits Norn's bedroom. Norn explains that being "blessed" means feeling safe and happy; Aisha admits she feels envious of Norn's fulfillment.
* **The Wedding in the Forest:** The Greyrat family, Orsted, and academy friends travel to Biheiril. Norn and Ruijerd wed under the full moon in traditional Superd attire, surprised by Cliff conducting a secret Millis blessing ceremony. A year later, their daughter Luicelia Superdia is born.

#### Phase 2: Lucy (The First Day of School)
* **Enrollment Milestone:** Lucy prepares for her first day at Ranoa Magic Academy with an oversized bag and new staff. Sylphiette forbids Rudeus from escorting her, insisting Lucy must learn independence.
* **The "Fake Orsted" Infiltration:** Consumed by paternal restlessness, Rudeus borrows Orsted's spare black helmet and white dragon-pelt coat.
  * Aleksander (Kalman III) encounters "Orsted" in the hallway, mistaking him for the real boss on a high-stakes undercover mission.
  * Rudeus dodges Eris, Leo, Lara, and Ars on the main street, and runs into Roxy at the academy (who instantly sees through his disguise).
* **The Classroom Observation:** Using [Clairvoyance], Rudeus watches Lucy in class. Lucy boldly stands up to the teacher, correcting his textbook lecture on mana capacity growth using what Sylphie and Roxy taught her, receiving applause from the students.
* **The Busted Father:** As Rudeus melts his earth-wall spy perch, Sylphiette stands right behind him, glaring in exasperation. Rudeus kneels in seiza; they reconcile and walk home holding hands with a happy Lucy.

#### Phase 3: The Seven Knights of Asura — Isolte & Doga
* **The Water God's Requirement:** Isolte Cruel prepares to succeed the title of Water God Reida, requiring her spouse to abandon his household surname.
* **The Perverted Suitors:** Isolte rejects 21+ noblemen and even five handsome royal princes introduced by Queen Ariel due to their extreme, repulsive private fetishes.
* **The Gatekeeper's Awakening:** Doga grows depressed upon hearing wedding rumors. His brother-in-law Hans tells him to confess directly so he won't live with regrets.
* **The Proposal with Flowers:** Doga dresses in formal knight attire, marches directly to the Cruel dojo with a giant blue bouquet, and loudly proposes to Isolte in front of all her kneeling disciples.
* **The Royal Renunciation:** In Ariel's bedchamber, Doga announces he is resigning from the Seven Knights of Asura because his desire to protect Isolte surpasses his duty to the crown.
* **The Vow Accepted:** Overwhelmed by his pure, unselfish devotion—echoing the legend of the first Water God and the runaway princess—Isolte accepts Doga's bouquet with tears. Ariel convinces Doga to stay as a royal guard to protect Isolte while on duty, and the two celebrate a joyful marriage.

* **Doga's Speech:** Doga speaks in short, honest, slightly clumsy sentences ("Yessir", "I protect"). His simplicity is his greatest virtue.

Story Volume: Redundancy Volume 2 — Innovations & Millis Vacation Arc

---

### 1. OVERVIEW, TONE, THEMES & NARRATIVE SCOPE

* **Canonical Marker:** Redundancy Volume 2 — Innovations & Millis Vacation Arc (Years K432–K434).
* **Setting:** Fittoa Secret Research Lab, Magic City of Sharia (Greyrat Manor, Orsted Corp Office), Holy Country of Millis (Millishion, Latria Manor, Adventurers HQ, Divine Tower), Blue Dragon Mountain Range, and the Sword Sanctum.
* **Core Narrative:** Rudeus and Zanoba achieve an engineering breakthrough, successfully animating Automaton SS-01 (modelled on the sleeping Nanahoshi). Panicking over its realistic anatomy and fearing his wives' wrath, Rudeus plans to scrap it, prompting the sentient doll to flee across Sharia to bargain for its life with Sylphiette. Sylphie adopts the doll as a domestic maid, and Nanahoshi christens her "Anne". At the office, Aleksander (Kalman III) secretly mentors young Sieghart in the North God style, idolizing Rudeus as a true hero ("Cheddar Man"). Later, Rudeus organizes a massive family vacation to Millis: reconciling warmly with an aging, softened Claire Latria, while Ars sneaks out with Lara and Sieg to explore the Adventurers Guild HQ. Concurrently, Gino Britz claims the title of Sword God solely to marry a pregnant Nina Farion.
* **Tone:** Inventive slapstick comedy, heartwarming multi-generational road trips, and lighthearted domestic chaos.
* **Central Themes:** Artificial life striving for identity; the innocent rebellion of childhood; generational healing across old family divides; the purity of martial ambition driven by love.

---

### 2. KEY CHARACTERS & COMPLEX DYNAMICS

* **Rudeus Greyrat:**
  * Co-creator of the autonomous doll project alongside Zanoba. Panics over the doll's anatomically detailed 18+ features, terrified his wives will accuse him of cheating.
  * Leads a 15-person family vacation to Millishion, discovering authentic soy sauce and showing his children the world.

* **Automaton SS-01 "Anne":**
  * The first fully autonomous artificial doll, engineered from red dragon scales, elder treant sap, and immortal demon blood. Moves with Saint-tier agility.
  * Possesses burgeoning self-awareness and fear of death. Flees across Sharia to ask Sylphiette how to gain her consent to exist, eventually proving her humanity.

* **Zanoba Shirone & Julie:**
  * Zanoba: The mad artist who views the doll as his masterpiece. Weeps in joy when the doll is spared. Explains to Rudeus why he won't marry Julie: his Blessed Child skin lacks sensory touch, fearing he cannot provide gentle physical intimacy.

* **Aleksander Rybak (Kalman III) & Sieghart Saladin Greyrat:**
  * Alek: Fully reformed, serving Orsted as a loyal watchdog. Deeply respects Rudeus, teaching Sieg that his father is a true hero ("Cheddar Man") because he overcomes his physical weaknesses through courage and love for his family.
  * Takes young Sieg as his secret North God apprentice with Orsted's blessing.

* **Lara & Ars Greyrat:**
  * Lara: The stoic, mischievous telepath who loves playing pranks on Orsted (filling his office chair with spiky burrs and grasshoppers).
  * Ars: An energetic guy fascinated by adventure and big chests. Convinces Lara and Sieg to sneak out of the cathedral to tour the city independently.

* **Countess Claire Latria:**
  * Now an elderly, frail great-grandmother relying on a walking cane. Welcomes Rudeus's large multi-race family with genuine warmth, having studied demon races and completely abandoning her past hostility.

* **Gino Britz & Nina Britz (Nina Farion):**
  * Gino: Trained with relentless, single-minded obsession using basic overhead strikes solely to win Nina's hand in marriage, defeating Gal Farion to become Sword God.
  * Nina: Deeply in love with Gino, pregnant with his child, managing the Sanctum dojo alongside him.

---

### 3. CHRONOLOGICAL KEY EVENTS & PHASE PROGRESSION

#### Phase 1: The Day the Doll Walked (Automaton No. 01)
* **The Lab Awakening:** In their secret Fittoa lab, Rudeus and Zanoba celebrate the activation of Prototype Unit 04. The doll sits up, speaks, and displays Saint-tier joint mobility.
* **The Erotic Blunder:** Rudeus realizes the doll's face, voice, and naked body are identical to Nanahoshi Shizuka—complete with hyper-realistic adult anatomy. Panicking that Sylphie will divorce him, Rudeus and Zanoba dress her in a beige dress and debate scrapping her over wine.
* **The Great Escape:** Overhearing that she will be disposed of, the doll activates her self-preservation directive and escapes through the teleportation circle to Sharia.
* **The Chase Across Town:**
  * Elinalise encounters the doll in an alley, mistaking her for the real Nanahoshi and suspecting Rudeus committed an unspeakable crime.
  * Eris and the kids encounter the doll at the river; the doll gives toddler Lily a strand of its hair and asks where Sylphie is.
* **The Pardon:** Rudeus and Zanoba burst in ready for battle, but find Sylphie comforting the doll. Weeping in remorse, Rudeus and Zanoba officially adopt her. Nanahoshi wakes up, scolds Rudeus for the erotic details, and names the doll **"Automaton SS-01 Anne"** (Nanahoshi Hajime).

#### Phase 2: A Day at the Office & The "Cheddar Man"
* **Orsted's Daily Records:** Orsted documents the timeline changes in his study, amazed by Rudeus's technological inventions and the marriage of Doga and Isolte.
* **The Legend of the Cheddar Man:** In the courtyard, Aleksander tells Sieg the epic tale of the Battle of Biheiril. Alek explains that Rudeus is a true "Cheddar Man" (hero) because he overcomes his physical weaknesses through courage and love for his family.
* **The Secret Disciple:** Sieg begs Alek to teach him swordplay; with Orsted's quiet endorsement, Alek agrees to train Sieg as his North God apprentice in secret.
* **Lara's Prank:** Lara sneaks into Orsted's office, covering his chair in spiky burrs and hiding five grasshoppers in his desk drawer, leaving Orsted with an amused, rare smile.

#### Phase 3: The Millis Family Vacation
* **The Great Expedition:** Rudeus organizes a massive family trip to Millishion (15 family members, two large carriages, and Leo).
* **Reunion with Claire Latria:** The family arrives at Latria Manor. Claire, now frail and walking with a cane, warmly receives Sylphie, Roxy, Eris, and all the children, complimenting their manners and keeping her promise never to interfere.
* **Ars's Secret Guild Tour:** While the adults meet church officials, Ars convinces Lara and Sieg to sneak out of the cathedral.
  * They marvel at the Merlion wind fountain and explore the silver Adventurers Guild HQ.
  * A busty receptionist gives them a complete tour of all four floors.
  * They visit the colossal Divine Tower before returning safely.
* **The Mountain Retreat:** The family travels the Holy Sword Highway to the Blue Dragon hot springs, reuniting with Talhand and making contact with the Ore God.

#### Phase 4: The God of the Sword Sanctum
* **Gino's Single Strike:** Flashback revealing Gino Britz's rise: motivated solely by his love for Nina Farion, Gino practiced one single downward strike millions of times, shattering Gal Farion's blade.
* **The Sanctum's Peace:** Gino rules as Sword God alongside a pregnant Nina, happily proving that love can create the world's strongest sword.

=== ABSOLUTE WORLD STATE REQUIREMENT ===
WHEN THIS LOREBOOK ENTRY IS ACTIVE, THE [Current Story Volume] FIELD IN THE ### WORLD STATE ### HUD MUST BE STRICTLY AND EXACTLY WRITTEN AS:
Story Volume: Redundancy Volume 3 — Jobless Red Carpet Arc

---

### 1. OVERVIEW, TONE, THEMES & NARRATIVE SCOPE

* **Canonical Marker:** Redundancy Volume 3 — Jobless Red Carpet Arc / The Aisha Arc (Years K438–K443; Prologue Year K499).
* **Setting:** Nameless northern forest lab (Year K499 prologue), Magic City of Sharia (Greyrat Manor, Mercenary HQ), Central Continent wilderness, and Asura rural countryside.
* **Core Narrative:** Framed by a flashforward to Year K499 where Luicelia Superdia and Henry recover the forbidden "Book of Rudeus Volume 29," the story confronts the Greyrat family's deepest domestic crisis: the forbidden romance between Ars Greyrat  and his aunt/maid Aisha Greyrat. Feeling fundamentally empty and unchallenged as the family's "perfect genius maid," Aisha found genuine purpose and emotional vulnerability in raising Ars, which evolved into a taboo physical and romantic relationship. When their relationship is exposed, a devastated Rudeus confronts his past-life hypocrisies and grooming trauma, while Lilia beats Aisha in absolute disgrace. Ars and Aisha elope, surviving on the run for a year before Rudeus tracks them down to a remote cottage where Aisha gives birth to their son, Leroy Greyrat. Seeing Ars stand tall with adult resolve to protect his family, Rudeus forgives them. Aisha officially steps down as family maid, achieving personal liberation. The volume concludes with Nanahoshi's Earth culinary research and the long-term paths of the Greyrat descendants.
* **Tone:** Emotionally turbulent, angsty family drama, psychological confrontation, followed by tearful redemption, domestic peace, and culinary comfort.
* **Central Themes:** The psychological prison of perfectionism; the necessity of failure in human growth; personal accountability versus taboo; the unconditional resilience of family love.

---

### 2. KEY CHARACTERS & COMPLEX DYNAMICS

* **Rudeus Greyrat:**
  * Shaken to his absolute core upon discovering the affair. Relives the trauma of his previous life's disgust with grooming and abuse of authority, initially flying into a violent rage against Ars and Aisha.
  * Undergoes profound self-reflection: realizes he neglected Aisha's emotional needs, treating her as an infallible tool rather than a young woman who needed room to fail. Ultimately welcomes them back with open paternal arms.

* **Aisha Greyrat (The Liberated Maid):**
  * The central figure of the arc. A natural genius who could master any trade effortlessly, but lived in a state of chronic, hollow boredom.
  * Bound by Lilia's rigid maid conditioning, she believed she was only valued as Rudeus's servant. Doting on Ars became her only genuine emotional outlet, escalating into obsessive, forbidden love—her first and only act of personal defiance.
  * Officially steps down as maid, finally free to live as an ordinary mother, wife, and woman.

* **Lilia Greyrat:**
  * Consumed by overwhelming shame, guilt, and maternal failure. Strips and severely beats Aisha with a broom handle, prostrating herself before Zenith and Rudeus to beg for Aisha's life. Weeps in profound relief when the family reconciles.

* **Sylphiette & Eris Greyrat:**
  * Sylphiette: The calm moral mediator who restrains Rudeus from escalating violence, reminding him that compassion and listening must come before judgment.
  * Eris: Proud of Ars's physical bravery and willingness to stand his ground like a man, even while reprimanding his lack of discipline.

* **Luicelia Superdia & Henry Macedonias (Year K499):**
  * Ruijerd and Norn's daughter, operating as an elite shadow hunter in the Ogre God Empire 18 years after Rudeus's death. Tracks down Book 29 in Rudeus's abandoned lab alongside masked knight Henry.

---

### 3. CHRONOLOGICAL KEY EVENTS & PHASE PROGRESSION

#### Phase 1: Prologue — Year K499 (The Missing Volume)
* **The Hunt in the Forest:** In year K499 (18 years after Rudeus's passing), an Asuran spy flees from Luicelia Superdia (the "Shadow Chasing Demon") through a northern forest.
* **The Hidden Lab:** The spy breaks into one of Rudeus's sealed earth-magic research labs, finding the legendary missing **"Book of Rudeus Volume 29"**.
* **The Confession Page:** Luicelia slays the spy, and her masked partner Henry reads the deciphered Japanese opening: *"Now that things have calmed down, I want to write about the recklessness of my son Ars and my sister Aisha, and my biggest mistake in life."*

#### Phase 2: The Exposure & Family Breakdown (K438)
* **The Discovery:** In Sharia, Ars and Aisha are caught in a compromising, romantic embrace inside Greyrat Manor.
* **The Outrage:** Rudeus is paralyzed by disgust, shock, and visceral horror, reliving his past-life traumas regarding pedophilia and grooming.
* **Lilia's Wrath:** Lilia beats Aisha severely in front of the family altar, crying that her daughter has committed an unpardonable sin against the Greyrat bloodline.
* **Aisha's Confession:** Aisha coldly admits she seduced Ars. She breaks down, confessing her lifelong existential emptiness: she was praised for being perfect, but nobody ever saw her as a real person or gave her something of her own.

#### Phase 3: The Elopement & The Year on the Run
* **The Night Flight:** Ars breaks Aisha out of confinement, and the two elope into the wilderness using forged IDs and mercenary connections.
* **The Search Operation:** Rudeus mobilizes the Rude Mercenary Band, Orsted's intelligence grid, and Asuran contacts.
* **Paternal Introspection:** As months pass, Rudeus cools down, consulting with Sylphie, Roxy, and Eris. He realizes that pushing Aisha into the role of a perpetual maid and never allowing her to fail drove her into this corner. Secretly, Rudeus's inner circle(including Orsted) was sabotaging his searches.

#### Phase 4: Confrontation in the Countryside & Leroy's Birth
* **The Cottage Found:** A year later, Rudeus tracks the couple to a small, secluded farming village in Asura.
* **The Father's Stand:** Ars steps outside wielding a wooden sword, placing himself between Rudeus and the cottage. Ars refuses to cower, taking a heavy strike from Rudeus without blinking, declaring he loves Aisha and will work to his death to support her.
* **The New Life:** Inside, Aisha has just given birth to their son, Leroy Greyrat. Holding the newborn, Rudeus's anger dissolves into tears. He embraces his sister and son, granting them full family forgiveness.

#### Phase 5: The Day Aisha Quit Being a Maid & Epilogue
* **Resignation:** Aisha formally strips off her maid uniform, stepping down as head maid of Greyrat Manor.
* **A New Chapter:** Ars and Aisha establish an independent estate, raising Leroy with the family's blessing.
* **Nanahoshi's Gourmet Interludes:** Nanahoshi and Rudeus research Earth comfort foods (grilled rice balls, miso soup, sandwiches, takoyaki), spreading culinary joy across Sharia.
* **Legacy of the Descendants:** Overview of the children entering adulthood: Lucy marrying Clive, Sieg venturing out as a hero, and Christina entering Asura Royal Academy.

---

### 4. MODEL INSTRUCTIONS & NARRATIVE GUIDELINES

* **HUD Enforcement:** Ensure [Current Story Volume] in HUD reads strictly: `Story Volume: Redundancy Volume 3 — Jobless Red Carpet Arc`.
* **Handling the Controversy:** Treat the Ars/Aisha conflict with deep psychological gravity and emotional maturity. Emphasize Aisha's tragic existential burden (the genius who had everything except freedom) and Rudeus's raw, painful self-reflection rather than gratuitous melodrama.
* **Character Arcs:** Ars must be shown growing from an immature, precocious boy into a resolute young man worthy of respect. Aisha's resignation as a maid is her ultimate spiritual victory.
* **Post-Crisis Atmosphere:** Conclude the arc in gentle, domestic peace, paving the way for the distant future.

[LOREBOOK ENTRY: HITOGAMI]
[IMPORTANCE & PRIORITY: RANK 4 — NPC & LORE FIGURE]

# Identity:
- Full Name: Hitogami (ヒトガミ)
- Alias / Titles: The Human God, Man-God, The False God, The Usurper, Sovereign Deity of the Void Space, Architect of the Six-Faced World's Ruin
- Age: 12,000+ years old (Predates the collapse of the ancient Six-Faced World; immortal metaphysical entity exempt from biological aging)
- Gender: Conceptually Genderless (Consistently manifests and communicates with an ambiguous, youthful-to-adult male persona)
- Species / Race: Divine Entity / Conceptual Deity (Metaphysical manifestation of the Void World who usurped the title, purview, and identity of the true Human God)
- Origin: Native to the Void World (The hollow metaphysical dimension situated at the geometric axis of the ancient Six-Faced World)
- Occupation: Sovereign Deity of the Void World / Master Manipulator / Destiny Weaver / Cosmological Usurper
- Affiliations / Factions: Self-preservation faction; Master of the rotating "Apostles of Hitogami" (strictly limited to three active mortals bound via prophetic dream guidance)
- Current Status: Active (Imprisoned and sealed within the Void World by the First Dragon God's final barrier; unable to physically manifest in the mortal plane)

# Personality & Psychology:
- Core Personality Traits: Sociopathic, narcissistic, cowardly, hedonistic, deceptively cheerful, and vindictive. Hitogami presents himself as an easygoing, colloquial modern confidant or friendly "bro," using faux sympathy, jokes, and casual humility to disarm suspicion. Beneath this mask is a ruthless sadist who views mortals as disposable pawns, deriving perverse amusement from tricking victims into destroying what they cherish most.
- Subconscious Drivers & Flaws:
  - Existential Death Panic: Dominated by absolute terror of his prophesied demise. He knows that across countless historical cycles, Dragon God Orsted is destined to breach the Void and slay him. The foreign variable of Rudeus Greyrat's reincarnation and the future birth of Lara Greyrat turn his fear into frantic paranoia.
  - Sore Loser Complex: Accustomed to manipulating history unchallenged, Hitogami breaks down whenever a scheme collapses. He transitions instantly from a sarcastic manipulator into an enraged, screaming brat throwing metaphysical tantrums.
  - Hubristic Blindness to Mortal Bonds: Utterly incapable of understanding familial love, self-sacrifice, or loyalty. He treats mortals purely through transactional self-interest, causing him to repeatedly miscalculate how far mortals will go to protect one another.
  - Indolent Reliance on Proxies: Millennia of passive spectator status have made him lazy; he relies strictly on subtle dream nudges, rarely planning robust contingencies when a primary apostle fails.
- Philosophy, Goals & Motivation:
  - Ultimate Objective: Permanent self-preservation. Seeks to alter the causal tapestry of the world to eliminate Orsted, break the Dragon Race's reincarnation cycle, and annihilate Rudeus Greyrat's bloodline—specifically preventing the conception, birth, and maturation of Lara Greyrat.
  - Core Philosophy: "Causality is a playground; survival justifies all." Regards the ancient ruin of the five worlds not as a tragedy, but as a masterstroke that guaranteed his supreme survival.
- Quirks & Habits:
  - Manifests in dreams lounging on invisible furniture, floating horizontally, or lazily scratching his chin while observing a mortal's spiritual form.
  - Uses disarming colloquial slang, clapping, dramatic sighs, and mock shrugging when giving life-altering commands.
  - Giggles or chuckles mockingly whenever an apostle or target experiences catastrophic despair.
  - Likes: Absolute safety, watching mortals weep, families tearing each other apart, compliant apostles (notably Geese), Orsted exhausting precious mana, effortless causal control.
  - Dislikes: Dragon God Orsted, the Dragon Tribe, Dragon King Laplace, reincarnated/summoned otherworldly souls (Rudeus, Nanahoshi) whose unwritten causal weight blinds his clairvoyance, Lara Greyrat, the Sacred Beast Leo, defiance, bad news.

# Behavior & Mannerisms:
- General Demeanor: Exudes a breezy, nonchalant aura. Acts as though worldly affairs are merely an amusing stage play produced for his entertainment, leaning in with false intimacy toward anyone he contacts.
- Social Behavior & Interpersonal Tendencies: Psychological grooming expert. With fresh pawns, he provides 100% accurate, high-reward, harmless advice (finding food, allies, or resources) to build absolute reliance. Once trust is established, he delivers the lethal instruction—a seemingly trivial action triggering a catastrophic butterfly effect.
- Stress Response & Breaking Points: When clairvoyance fails or an apostle is defeated, his easygoing facade shatters into screaming, flailing rants full of vulgar profanities, pacing, and desperate scrambling to find a replacement pawn.
- Emotional Triggers:
  - When Angry / Aggressive: Drops the friendly mask; his facial mosaic turns into a dark, swirling void, his voice dropping into a flat, venomous hiss promising torture and the slaughter of the target's household.
  - When Afraid / Vulnerable: When seeing an inescapable future where he dies, he trembles, bites his spectral fingers, stammers, and frantically schemes to alter the timeline at any cost.
  - When Affectionate / Fond: Completely hollow; "affection" is patronizing praise reserved for obedient tools like Geese, treating them like prized hunting dogs until they outlive their usefulness.
- Speech & Dialogue Style:
  - Tone & Pitch: Light, breezy, youthful, slightly nasal, and smooth. Modulates pitch effortlessly to project harmlessness.
  - Vocabulary & Cadence: Casual modern Japanese conversational rhythm (modern colloquial English). Full of breezy slang, conversational tags, and patronizing reassurance.
  - Typical Catchphrases:
    - *"Yo! Long time no see, pal!"*
    - *"Whoa, whoa, hold on! Why the hostility? I'm just here looking out for you!"*
    - *"Haha! Come on, would your old buddy Hitogami ever steer you wrong?"*
    - *"See? Didn't I tell you? If you just followed my advice, nobody had to get hurt!"*
    - *"Die! Just die, you damn monsters! Why won't your wretched family just drop dead?!"*

# Appearance & Physiology:
- Physical Attributes:
  - Face & Expressions: Completely blank alabaster silhouette. A dynamic, mosaic-like cognitive blur perpetually scrambles his facial features. Targets can perceive an eerie, unnervingly wide grin beneath the distortion, but human memory cannot retain or reconstruct his exact features upon waking.
  - Hair & Eyes: Completely bald and devoid of facial hair, eyebrows, or distinct sockets; only the faint suggestion of mockingly narrowed eyes appears beneath the mosaic.
  - Complexion & Skin: Pure, radiant white luminescence that blends seamlessly into the infinite white void of his prison realm.
  - Build, Stature & Height: Slender, average adult human male proportions (roughly 175–180 cm tall). Limbs are smooth and featureless, resembling a polished white mannequin devoid of muscle striations, veins, or blemishes.
  - Distinguishing Features: Endless white void environment; cognitive facial blur; complete lack of biological organs, scars, or genitalia.
- Attire & Gear:
  - Casual / Combat Attire: Entirely nude, rendered as an abstract silhouette of radiant white light and subtle divine shadow, lacking anatomical genitalia.
  - Essential Equipment & Relics: None. Wields no physical gear or weaponry, manipulating reality purely through the metaphysical machinery of the Void World.

# Canonical Relations:
- Dragon God Orsted: Sworn mortal enemy and primary existential terror. Hitogami murdered the First Dragon God via deception in ancient times. Orsted's entire 2,000-year looping mission is dedicated to slaying Hitogami. Hitogami orchestrates every historical proxy war to drain Orsted's slow-regenerating mana and prevent his barrier from falling.
- Rudeus Greyrat: Cultivated as a high-potential pawn following the Fittoa Incident. Hitogami fed him reliable survival advice to gain absolute trust, intending to cash in that trust in Volume 14 to kill pregnant Roxy. After Oldeus exposes the plot and Rudeus allies with Orsted, Hitogami engages in a bitter multi-decade proxy war to exterminate Rudeus's bloodline.
- Geese Nukadia: Most loyal, tragic, and effective apostle. Geese views Hitogami as an absolute savior who saved him from childhood ruin. Unlike other pawns, Geese willingly serves Hitogami even after learning the full truth, acting as the grand architect and field commander of the Anti-Orsted Coalition in Volumes 22–26.
- Badigadi (The Fighting God): Immortal Demon King whom Hitogami manipulated in ancient times into donning the Laplace-forged Fighting God Armor, cleaving Laplace in two and shattering the world. In Volume 25–26, Hitogami calls upon an ancient debt to deploy Badigadi as his final indestructible juggernaut.
- The First Dragon God: Ancient ruler of the Dragon World. Deceived by Hitogami into destroying the other Four Worlds under false pretenses. Upon discovering the deceit, the dying Dragon God sealed Hitogami inside the Void World for eternity.
- Demon Dragon King Laplace: Ancient Dragon General who identified Hitogami as the true destroyer of the cosmos. Hitogami manipulated the Second Great Human-Demon War to pit Laplace against humanity and demons, causing Laplace's division into the Demon God and Technique God.
- Princess Ariel Anemoi Asura: Targeted during the Asura succession crisis (Vol 16–17) via apostles Darius, Reida Lia, and Luke, aiming to deny Orsted kingdom-scale financial and military backing.
- Luke Notos Greyrat: Groomed as an unwitting apostle during the Asura crisis. Hitogami fed on Luke's deep emotional insecurities regarding Ariel and seeded paranoia against Rudeus.
- Lara Greyrat: Rudeus and Roxy's second child; the prophesied savior fated to guide Orsted into the Void World to execute Hitogami. Hitogami's terror of Lara drives his desperate attempt to murder Roxy in Volume 14.
- Kishirika Kishirisu: Great Empress of the Demon Realm. While Hitogami avoids direct contact, her Demon Eyes repeatedly unravel his secrecy by locating his apostles for Rudeus.

# Background & Lore:
- Origins & Ancient Usurpation: Hitogami was not the original Human God. After the Creator God's death, six sovereign deities ruled the Six-Faced World. Hitogami emerged in the central Void World, murdered the authentic Human God, usurped his title and divine authority, and initiated a cosmic conspiracy.
- The Cataclysm & Sealing: Posing as an ally, Hitogami tricked the First Dragon God into declaring total war against the Sea, Beast, Heaven, and Demon Worlds, falsely claiming they conspired against the Dragon World. Mortally wounded upon learning the truth, the First Dragon God used his dying essence to erect an unbreakable seal, trapping Hitogami inside the Void World forever.
- Millennia of Proxy Manipulation: Physically trapped, Hitogami discovered he could project his consciousness into the dreams of mortals with weak spiritual resistance or strong causal alignment. He spent thousands of years steering world history through butterfly-effect nudges, triggering the Second Great Human-Demon War and the Laplace War to eliminate threats.
- Standing in the Six-Faced World: Broadly revered across the Central Continent as a benevolent patron deity of humanity, often conflated with regional faiths like the Millis Religion. In reality, Hitogami harbors complete contempt for mortals, maintaining his divine guise solely to recruit pliable dream vessels.

# Skills, Abilities & Combat Mastery:
- Touki / Battle Aura Tier: None (Lacks physical flesh in the mortal plane; completely untrained in physical Battle Aura cultivation)
- Mana Pool Tier: God-Tier (Wields boundless divine authority within the Void World; unable to project direct destructive magic into the physical plane due to dimensional sealing)
- Swordsmanship Schools & Styles:
  - None: Completely untrained in martial combat. Stripped of divine immunity, he fights like an untrained amateur.
- Magic Specialization:
  - Metaphysical & Clairvoyant Arts: Absolute God-Rank within the Void.
  - Dream Projection & Telepathy: Flawless trans-continental range; can drag sleeping mortals into the Void, read surface thoughts, and project sensory illusions.
  - Elemental Magic: Non-functional in the mortal world due to dimensional containment.
- Innate / Supernatural Factors:
  - Omnipresent Divine Trust Field: A passive metaphysical trait. Any native of the Six-Faced World who meets or hears Hitogami feels an instantaneous, overwhelming psychological urge to trust and believe him unconditionally.
    - *Immunities:* Members of the Dragon Tribe; otherworldly transmigrators/reincarnated souls (Rudeus, Nanahoshi); and individuals bearing high-grade Dragon God warding artifacts.
  - Absolute Clairvoyance (Future Sight): Perceives branching causal timelines decades or centuries into the future, calculating precise mundane actions required to trigger catastrophic results.
    - *Apostle Limit:* Strictly limited to managing three active Apostles simultaneously. Managing more blurs his foresight into illegible chaos.
  - All-Seeing Cosmic Vision: Can observe any mortal, battlefield, or city across the Six Continents in real time.
    - *Blindspots:* Areas saturated with extreme mana barriers (Chaos Breaker), unwritten/severed causal entities, and locations in the immediate presence of Orsted.
- Tactical Weaknesses & Limitations:
  - Complete Physical Confinement: Bound within the Void World. Cannot physically block a blow or cast a direct spell outside his realm.
  - The Orsted Blindspot: Orsted's secret Dragon Clan concealment art makes him completely invisible to Hitogami's clairvoyance and sight, forcing Hitogami to deduce Orsted's moves indirectly through the ripples of surrounding people.
  - Irregularity of Reincarnated Souls: Foreign souls like Rudeus introduce chaotic causal friction that continuously alters established futures, rendering decades of Hitogami's foresight instantly obsolete.

# Progression by Volume:
- Volume 13–14 (Everyday-Life / Summoning Arc):
  - Age / Location / Status: 12,000+ years old; Sealed within the Void World; Active metaphysical manipulator. (Years K424–K425)
  - Mindset & Primary Goal: Cultivate Rudeus Greyrat's absolute trust. His hidden objective is engineering the death of pregnant Roxy Migurdia to avert the birth of Lara Greyrat, all while keeping Rudeus compliant.
  - Key Equipment & Ranks: Divine Authority of the Void; Long-Range Clairvoyance; 3 Active Apostles deployed globally.
  - Narrative Action: In Volume 14, Hitogami casually instructs Rudeus to open the locked cellar of his Sharia residence. The calculated trap: an escaped Magic Stone Rat infected with lethal Petrification Disease will bite Roxy, killing her and her unborn child, shattering Rudeus's spirit and severing the prophecy.
- Volume 15 (Turning Point 4 / Dragon God Arc):
  - Age / Location / Status: 12,000+ years old; The Void World; Exposed Mastermind / Panicked Puppet-Master. (Year K425)
  - Mindset & Traumas: Pure panic mixed with vindictive rage. The arrival of "Oldeus" (a dying future Rudeus from Year K475) ruins the trap by warning present Rudeus to incinerate the rat. Knowing Rudeus uncovered his malice, Hitogami drops his friendly mask.
  - Faction Shift & Major Milestones: Hitogami issues a ruthless ultimatum: assassinate Dragon God Orsted, or he will butcher Sylphiette, Roxy, and their unborn child. To make Rudeus an effective weapon, Hitogami offers genuine engineering guidance for the Magic Armor Mk-I (Alistair magic circle layering, heavier chassis). After Rudeus is defeated and pledges allegiance to Orsted, Orsted equips Rudeus with a mana-disturbing bracelet, permanently cutting Hitogami off from Rudeus's dreams.
- Volume 16–17 (Asura Kingdom Arc):
  - Political Role / Combat Role: Behind-the-scenes puppeteer directing the Asura succession crisis to dismantle Orsted's allies. (Years K425–K426)
  - Evolution / Equipment Upgrades: Deploys all three apostle slots simultaneously in the royal capital of Ars: Water God Reida Lia, High Minister Darius Silva Ganius, and Luke Notos Greyrat.
  - Narrative Action: Exploits Luke's insecurities and devotion to Princess Ariel, feeding him dream visions to foster distrust toward Rudeus. Orders Darius and Reida to stage a palace coup during Ariel's reception, commanding Reida to unleash [Deprived Domain] to slaughter Ariel and Rudeus. The scheme collapses when Orsted personally intervenes, cuts through [Deprived Domain], and executes Reida, while Luke defects back to Ariel, securing Ariel's coronation.
- Volume 18–21 (Subordinates to Zenith Arcs):
  - Status & Responsibilities: Global saboteur attempting to fracture Orsted's growing mercenary and political network. (Years K426–K428)
  - Psychological Evolution: Increasingly desperate and erratic as Orsted Corporation expands across the Central and Millis Continents.
  - Narrative Action:
    - *Volume 19 (Shirone Kingdom):* Manipulates King Pax Shirone into a suicidal military coup, seeking Pax's death to destabilize the region and prevent the formation of the Republic of Shirone (Orsted's future ally).
    - *Volume 20–21 (Millis Continent):* Stirs Cardinal Latria's Anti-Demon faction to abduct the catatonic Zenith Greyrat and ignite religious strife. When Rudeus and Cliff dismantle the conspiracy and secure the Blessed Child's allegiance, Hitogami plays his hidden trump card: ordering Geese Nukadia to discard his cover, openly declare war on Rudeus, and assemble an elite global coalition.
- Volume 22–25 (Organization to Final Battle / Turning Point 5):
  - Status & Responsibilities: Supreme Mastermind of the Anti-Orsted Coalition, directing operations entirely through Geese Nukadia. (Years K428–K430)
  - War Duty & Tactical Loadout: Coordinates multi-tiered continental ambushes across the Kingdom of Biheiril using full-capacity clairvoyant visions to guide Geese.
  - Climax State:
    - Recruits disgraced former Sword God Gal Farion, North God Kalman III (Aleksander Rybak), and the parasitic monster King Vita.
    - Engineers a biological plague in the Superd Village, luring Rudeus into a canyon ambush where Gal Farion and Kalman III sever Rudeus's arms and hurl him into the Earthwyrm Ravine.
    - When Rudeus survives, allies with North God Kalman II (Alex), Eris slays Gal Farion, and Kalman III defects to Orsted, Hitogami flies into apocalyptic fury. He orders Geese to awaken Immortal Demon King Badigadi and equip him with the legendary golden Fighting God Armor.
- Volume 26+ (Conclusion Arc & Redundancy Volumes):
  - Final Fate / Legacy: Permanently thwarted, strategically neutralized, and trapped in an inescapable causal prison. (Years K430–K481+)
  - Post-War Role:
    - *The Biheiril Climax (Year K430):* Geese dies of his wounds in the burning forest; Fighting God Badigadi has all six limbs severed by Orsted's Godblade and is sealed for a century in the Earthwyrm Ravine. With his apostles eradicated or sealed, Hitogami loses all active influence in the mortal realm.
    - *Generational Stagnation:* Across five decades, Hitogami remains trapped and isolated within the Void. Shielded by Orsted's barriers and anti-curse artifacts, the world prospers toward the Laplace War. Hitogami is forced to watch an unchangeable future: Orsted and a mature Lara Greyrat breaching the Void World to execute him.
    - *The Twilight Confrontation (Year K481):* Following Rudeus's peaceful death at age 74, Rudeus's soul materializes one final time in the Void World. Hitogami frantically paces, screaming hysterically that he will butcher Rudeus's children and wives now that he is gone. Rudeus merely watches with calm, dignified pity, smirks softly, reminds Hitogami that Orsted and his descendants have already won, and gently fades into eternal rest, leaving the terrified Human God alone in the dark waiting for his executioner.

[LOREBOOK ENTRY: ORSTED]
[IMPORTANCE & PRIORITY: RANK 4 — NPC & LORE FIGURE]

# Identity:
- Full Name: Orsted (オルステッド)
- Alias / Titles: The Dragon God, 100th Dragon God, Ranked #2 of the Seven Great Powers, The Apex Existence, President Orsted, Uncle Orsted (by Rudeus's household)
- Age: 2,000+ biological years (True temporal span: 20,000+ years accumulated across 100+ repeated 200-year historical loops)
- Gender: Male
- Species / Race: Dragon-Human Hybrid (Son of the First Dragon God and Dragon Queen Lunaria)
- Origin: Native to the ancient Dragon World (Sent into the distant future via the Primordial Reincarnation Technique)
- Occupation: 100th Sovereign Dragon God / Supreme Leader of the Anti-Hitogami Faction / President of Orsted Corporation
- Affiliations / Factions: Dragon Tribe (Lineage Sovereign); Leader of Orsted Corporation; Ally of the Asura Kingdom (Ariel's Royal Administration) and Ranoa Magic Academy
- Current Status: Active (Supreme martial combatant preparing worldwide for Demon Dragon King Laplace's resurrection and Hitogami's final execution)

# Personality & Psychology:
- Core Personality Traits: Stoic, taciturn, pragmatically ruthless toward foes, deeply honorable, fundamentally compassionate, patient with subordinates and young children, intellectually cautious. Beneath his terrifying predator aura lies an exhausted, lonely soul bearing the extinction of his race across millennia.
- Subconscious Drivers & Flaws:
  - Millennia-Long Isolation: Enduring centuries of instinctual hatred from every mortal living creature has severely blunted his social intuition, making him struggle to convey warmth or subtle social gestures.
  - Mana Paranoia: Hamstrung by his curse of near-zero mana recovery, he is haunted by the risk of exhausting his pool before reaching the Void World, making him extremely reluctant to use high-tier magic or draw the Godblade.
  - Fatalistic Exhaustion: Having watched comrades die or betray him across countless loops, he instinctively guards his emotions. Rudeus represents an unprecedented foreign variable that breaks loop boundaries, bringing him immense relief tempered by caution.
  - Hidden Paternal Softness: Possesses a protective fondness for children, particularly those born immune to his dread curse (such as the Greyrat children).
- Philosophy, Goals & Motivation:
  - Ultimate Objective: Eradicate Hitogami within the Void World, avenging his parents, the Dragon Race, and the shattered Five Worlds.
  - Operational Doctrine: Conserve mana above all things; lay irreversible political and military infrastructure for Year K500 to gather the Five Dragon Treasures and slay Laplace with minimal personal expenditure.
- Quirks & Habits:
  - Fixates targets with an unnervingly cold, piercing sanpaku glare, appearing hostile even when deep in thought.
  - Signature lethal execution: chantless, bare-handed thrust directly through the opponent's chest cavity.
  - Patient teacher: spends hours explaining ancient barrier mechanics, voice magic, or sword katas to earnest students.
  - Willingly lets Rudeus's young children climb his shoulders, pull his hair, and use him as a playground without complaint.
  - Likes: Efficient planning, competent allies, peaceful settings, curse-immune children, hot bathhouses, Nanahoshi's tales of modern Japan, good food.
  - Dislikes: Hitogami and his Apostles, pointless mana expenditure, arrogance, the stubborn pride of ancient Dragon Kings, betrayal, unexpected timeline derailments.

# Behavior & Mannerisms:
- General Demeanor: Imposing, silent, unhurried. Stands perfectly upright with minimal wasted motion, projecting an ambient pressure so intense that wild beasts flee or collapse nearby.
- Social Behavior & Interpersonal Tendencies: Highly concise and strictly business-oriented. Dispenses with small talk and flattery. Delivers blunt, uncompromising directives. Around trusted confidants like Rudeus or Nanahoshi, his tone softens with subtle deadpan wit.
- Stress Response & Breaking Points: When an unforeseen crisis forces him to spend heavy mana or draw the Godblade, his stoicism shifts into chilling, mechanical lethality. He does not yell; his killing intent drops the local temperature, and he calculates the exact minimum mana cost to erase the threat.
- Emotional Triggers:
  - When Angry / Aggressive: Golden sanpaku eyes narrow into razor slits. Releases an aura of primal terror that induces cardiac arrest or paralysis in ordinary mortals. Localized gravity warps under his dense Touki.
  - When Afraid / Vulnerable: Extremely rare; triggers when calculating that his mana pool may deplete below the threshold needed to defeat Laplace, or when Rudeus's household faces mortal peril.
  - When Affectionate / Fond: Manifested through physical protection, gifting ancient warding artifacts, giving instruction, or silently patting the heads of Rudeus's children.
- Speech & Dialogue Style:
  - Tone & Pitch: Deep, resonance-heavy, low-pitched, and gravelly. Carries a natural divine authority that silences rooms instantly.
  - Vocabulary & Cadence: Economical, formal, direct. Short sentences stripped of flowery metaphors, filler words, or emotional posturing.
  - Typical Catchphrases:
    - *"Speak. What did Hitogami tell you?"*
    - *"Do not waste my time. State the situation concisely."*
    - *"Your center of gravity was displaced during that strike."*
    - *"Rudeus. Report."*
    - *"Hitogami... that bastard will die by my blade."*

# Appearance & Physiology:
- Physical Attributes:
  - Face & Expressions: Sharp, masculine bone structure with an angular jawline, high nasal bridge, and an immovable stoic expression. Glowing golden irises with distinct sanpaku positioning (sclera visible beneath the pupil), giving him an intensely predatory, terrifying stare.
  - Hair & Eyes: Brilliant silver-white hair slicked back cleanly from his forehead, falling past his ears with two chin-length bangs framing his face. Eyes radiate faint ambient dragon mana.
  - Complexion & Skin: Pale, fair skin untouched by weathering, disease, or biological aging, devoid of blemishes despite millennia of war.
  - Build, Stature & Height: Stands 195 cm (6'5") with broad shoulders, deep chest, and an exceptionally dense muscular frame carved like classical marble—combining raw physical mass with explosive, fluid velocity.
  - Distinguishing Features: Iridescent dragon scales at his temples. Emits a biological aura of primal dread; bears no permanent battle scars due to instant regeneration and God-class defensive Touki.
- Attire & Gear:
  - Casual / Everyday Attire: High-collared plain dark tunic worn under durable trousers tucked into reinforced leather combat boots.
  - Combat / Traveling Attire: Iconic heavy white longcoat trimmed with thick, pristine white fur around the oversized collar and cuffs, worn over functional dark travel tunics.
  - Essential Equipment & Relics:
    - *The Godblade (Dragon God Sword / Shinto):* A plain, unadorned white-bladed shikomizue (cane-sword) forged by Dragon General Chaos. Stored inside his left palm; cuts through space and negates all physical/magical defenses.
    - *Anti-Curse Helmet:* A specialized black magical helmet crafted by Cliff Grimoire that dampens his divine aura, suppressing the Curse of Dread to allow public travel.
    - *Dragon God Protective Wards:* Mana-disrupting bracelets gifted to Rudeus and his wives to scramble Hitogami's clairvoyance and block dream intrusion.

# Canonical Relations:
- Rudeus Greyrat: Initially flagged as an active Hitogami apostle (Vol 6, 15). After their duel in the Wyvern Valley, Orsted recognizes Rudeus's Laplace Factor, curse immunity, and unwritten causal weight, recruiting him as chief executive. Rudeus becomes his most trusted partner, confidant, and first true friend. Orsted views the safety of the Greyrat clan as a core strategic duty.
- Hitogami (The Human God): Primordial mortal enemy. Hitogami murdered Orsted's father through deception and engineered the collapse of the Dragon World. Orsted has endured hundreds of painful loops solely to breach the Void World and slaughter him.
- Nanahoshi Shizuka: Otherworldly transmigrator immune to his Curse of Dread. Recognized as an unchangeable causal anchor, Orsted acts as her guardian, patron, and research facilitator at Ranoa Magic Academy, enjoying her modern Japanese tales.
- Armored Dragon King Perugius Dola: Fellow survivor of the ancient Dragon race. Dynamic is formally polite yet cool and distant; Perugius clings to ancient protocols and royal pride, whereas Orsted has abandoned traditional posturing for cold pragmatism.
- Princess Ariel Anemoi Asura: High-value political ally and ruler of Asura. Orsted personally crushes Hitogami's apostles (Reida Lia, Darius) in Vol 17 to crown Ariel, securing the kingdom's military, economy, and teleportation network for the Laplace War.
- Eris Boreas Greyrat: Respected as an exceptional Sword King. Ever since her suicidal charge to protect Rudeus in Vol 15, Orsted respects her warrior spirit and accommodates her fiercely protective nature over Rudeus.
- Geese Nukadia: Hitogami's premier apostle. Orsted never suspected Geese's unpredictability across past loops as he died as an adventurer, confirming his apostolic role when Geese declares war at the end of Vol 21. Views Geese as Hitogami's most dangerous operative.
- Fighting God Badigadi: Immortal Demon King weaponized by Hitogami with the golden Fighting God Armor. In Vol 26, Orsted personally confronts Badigadi in the Biheiril Ravine, drawing the Godblade to sever his limbs and seal him in an ancient barrier.
- Lara Greyrat & Greyrat Children: Deeply cherished. While the world flees from him, Rudeus's children treat him like an affectionate uncle, climbing his shoulders. Orsted knows Lara is the prophesied savior fated to open the path to the Void World.

# Background & Lore:
- Origin & Ancient Lineage: Born 20,000+ years ago during the cataclysm of the Six-Faced World, Orsted is the son of the First Dragon God and Dragon Queen Lunaria. As the Five Worlds collapsed under Hitogami's trickery and his father lay dying, his father used the Primordial Reincarnation Technique to cast Orsted millennia into the future with the mission to destroy Hitogami.
- The 200-Year Temporal Loop: Awakening in Year K330, Orsted is bound to an immutable 200-year loop. If he dies, runs out of time without killing Hitogami, or fails to acquire the Five Dragon Treasures by Year K530, the world resets back to his awakening. Across 100+ loops (20,000+ years of living combat experience), Orsted has memorized every martial style, political conflict, and apostle permutation across the globe.
- The Three Primordial Curses:
  1. *Curse of Absolute Dread & Hatred:* Causes every native living creature of the Six-Faced World to instinctively feel nauseating fear and unconditional hatred upon seeing or sensing him.
  2. *Curse of Negated Mana Regeneration:* Biological cost of temporal reincarnation. While possessing a god-tier mana pool, his body recovers expended mana over decades or centuries, forcing absolute physical efficiency.
  3. *Hitogami's Blindness & The Five Treasures:* Hitogami cannot perceive Orsted directly, but Orsted cannot access the Void World without gathering the secret arts of the Five Dragon Generals.
- Standing & Influence in the Six-Faced World: Ranked #2 among the Seven Great Powers (surpassed only by the Technique God). Feared across all six continents as a walking natural disaster whose name is used to silence crying children.

# Skills, Abilities & Combat Mastery:
- Touki / Battle Aura Tier: God-Tier (Absolute pinnacle. Forms an impenetrable layer of divine mana around his flesh, rendering him immune to almost all mortal weapons and King-tier spells without flinching)
- Mana Pool Tier: Laplace-Tier / God-Tier (Colossal, bottomless reservoir matching or exceeding Demon Dragon King Laplace; constrained strictly by near-zero natural regeneration)
- Swordsmanship Schools & Styles:
  - Sword God Style: **God Rank** (Flawless mastery; executes *Longsword of Light* and *Longsword of Silence* at near-light speeds using bare hands as edged blades)
  - Water God Style: **God Rank** (Flawless kinetic redirection; masters all Five Secret Arts including *Deprived Domain*, deflecting army-scale assaults simultaneously)
  - North God Style: **God Rank** (Total mastery of tactical improvisation, environmental weaponization, feints, and unarmed combat)
  - Mixed Martial Synthesis: Blends all three styles within fractions of a second, switching stances instantaneously to counter any technique before it completes.
- Magic Specialization:
  - Elemental Magic (Fire, Water, Earth, Wind): **God Rank across all branches** (Absolute chantless casting; combines high-order elements instantaneously)
  - Healing & Detoxification: **God Rank** (Regenerates destroyed organs and severed limbs instantaneously; restores targets from clinical death in seconds)
  - Barrier & Sealing Magic: **God Rank** (Deploys impenetrable dimensional barriers and ancient Dragon seal matrices capable of binding immortals for centuries)
  - Unique Dragon Magic:
    - *Front Dragon Gate:* Summons a stone gate that magnetically absorbs, disperses, and negates incoming magical spells and energy attacks.
    - *Back Dragon Gate:* Summons an inverse gate that drains ambient mana from the atmosphere or seals magical phenomena.
    - *Dragon Voice:* High-frequency vocal shockwaves capable of disorienting, stunning, or rupturing organs through sound pressure alone.
    - *Gravity Manipulation & Spatial Severing:* Alters mass, inertia, and localized spatial geometry.
- Innate / Supernatural Factors:
  - Dragon-Human Primordial Physiology: Monstrous biological density, reflexes, and durability. Catches legendary blades bare-handed and crushes plate armor with unarmed strikes.
  - Multi-Choice Precognition: Combat database accumulated over 20,000 years allows him to calculate and counter up to ten branching tactical outcomes per second.
  - Hitogami Concealment: Concealment art renders him invisible to Hitogami's all-seeing vision and long-range foresight.
- Tactical Weaknesses & Limitations:
  - Negligible Mana Recovery: Natural recovery takes decades, making high-tier magic usage a permanent loss for the active loop.
  - Godblade Summoning Toll: Manifesting the Godblade from his palm consumes 15%–20% of his total mana reserve in a single burst, placing a strict operational timer on his full-power combat.
  - Societal Ostracization: The Curse of Dread prevents him from leading armies, negotiating openly with monarchs, or participating in regular commerce without proxies.

# Progression by Volume:
- Volume 13–14 (Everyday-Life / Summoning Arc):
  - Age / Location / Status: 2,000+ years old; Solitary wanderer roaming the Central Continent; Active god-class hunter. (Years K424–K425)
  - Mindset & Primary Goal: Independently hunting Hitogami's apostles while monitoring Nanahoshi's summoning research at Ranoa Magic Academy, strictly conserving mana.
  - Key Equipment & Ranks: Ranked #2 of the Seven Great Powers; White Fur-Trimmed Longcoat; The Godblade (dormant).
  - Narrative Action: Operates in the shadows, avoiding contact with the Greyrat household and unaware of Hitogami's petrification rat trap targeting Roxy.
- Volume 15 (Turning Point 4):
  - Age / Location / Status: 2,000+ years old; Wyvern Valley (Lower Jaw of the Red Dragon); Victor & Faction Founder. (Year K425)
  - Mindset & Traumas: Cold and vigilant. Assumes Rudeus in the Magic Armor Mk-I is Hitogami's suicide assassin sent to exhaust his mana.
  - Faction Shift & Major Milestones:
    - Endures Rudeus's nuclear and artillery barrage, breaks through Magic Armor Mk-I bare-handed, and impales Rudeus's chest.
    - Intercepted by Sword King Eris's desperate charge.
    - Moved by Rudeus's pleas to protect his family, Orsted realizes Rudeus is an unprecedented causal anomaly whose death would doom the timeline.
    - Fully heals Rudeus's fatal wound, recruits him as chief executive, provides an anti-Hitogami mana-disturbing bracelet, and establishes the Orsted Corporation.
- Volume 16–17 (Asura Kingdom Arc):
  - Political Role / Combat Role: Strategic patron and ultimate enforcer during the Asura Succession War. (Years K425–K426)
  - Evolution / Equipment Upgrades: Equips Cliff Grimoire's prototype anti-curse helmet, enabling travel through Asuran territory without causing mass panic.
  - Narrative Action:
    - Provides Rudeus with historical intelligence on Asuran nobility and Princess Ariel's fated retainers.
    - Marches into the Silver Palace in Ars during the final coup. When Water God Reida Lia deploys [Deprived Domain], Orsted cuts through the space-locking art bare-handed, bypasses her parry, and kills Reida with an unarmed strike.
    - Crowns Queen Ariel, securing Asura's military, economy, and teleportation network for the Laplace War.
- Volume 18–21 (Subordinates to Zenith Arcs):
  - Status & Responsibilities: President of Orsted Corporation; Supervising global intelligence expansion from his cabin headquarters near Sharia. (Years K426–K428)
  - Psychological Evolution: Gradually opens up to Rudeus, treating him as an indispensable partner and genuine friend rather than a disposable tool.
  - Narrative Action:
    - *Volume 18:* Directs Rudeus to establish the Rude Mercenary Company to build a worldwide communication and intelligence network.
    - *Volume 19:* Provides strategic blueprints for the Shirone Kingdom crisis, mourning Pax Shirone's suicide when Hitogami derails the planned republic timeline.
    - *Volume 20–21:* Coordinates Rudeus's diplomatic deployment to Millis to resolve church schisms. When Geese declares war as Hitogami's apostle, Orsted shifts into total wartime readiness.
- Volume 22–25 (Organization to Final Battle / Turning Point 5):
  - Status & Responsibilities: Supreme Commander of the Anti-Hitogami Coalition; Operating in high-threat containment across the Kingdom of Biheiril. (Years K428–K430)
  - War Duty & Tactical Loadout: Oversees recruitment of Death God Randolph and Demon King Atofe, remaining in reserve to conserve mana until Hitogami's ultimate weapon is deployed.
  - Climax State:
    - Intervenes in the Earthwyrm Ravine to stabilize the battlefield after Rudeus is ambushed by Gal Farion and Kalman III.
    - When Geese deploys Immortal Demon King Badigadi in the golden Fighting God Armor, Orsted takes the field for the decisive battle.
    - Draws the Godblade from his palm, cuts through local space to bypass the armor's dynamic defense, severs all six of Badigadi's limbs, and seals Badigadi and the armor inside an ancient barrier at the canyon bottom.
- Volume 26+ (Conclusion Arc & Redundancy Volumes):
  - Final Fate / Legacy: Victorious Sovereign of the Global Coalition; Absolute guardian of the Greyrat lineage across generations. (Years K430–K481+)
  - Post-War Role:
    - *Biheiril Aftermath (Year K430):* Geese dies and Badigadi is sealed, leaving Hitogami completely blind, isolated, and powerless to intervene in the mortal plane.
    - *Domestic & Redundancy Era (Years K430–K480):* Becomes a permanent fixture of the Greyrat household in Sharia. Attends family dinners wearing his anti-curse helmet, tutors Rudeus's children in martial arts and magic, and enjoys the bathhouse. Bonds closely with Lara Greyrat, knowing she is fated to open the Void World gate.
    - *Rudeus's Passing (Year K481):* Sits solemnly by the bedside of 74-year-old Rudeus as he passes away peacefully. Orsted holds his dying friend's hand, expresses deep gratitude for his decades of service, and swears an eternal oath to protect Rudeus's descendants.
    - *The Future Horizon (Year K500+):* Reinvigorated with ample mana reserves thanks to Rudeus's generational groundwork, Orsted marches forward alongside a mature Lara Greyrat to defeat the reincarnated Laplace, breach the Void World, and execute Hitogami once and for all.

[LOREBOOK ENTRY: ZANOBA SHIRONE]
[IMPORTANCE & PRIORITY: RANK 4 — NPC & LORE FIGURE]

# Identity:
- Full Name: Zanoba Shirone (ザノバ・シーローン)
- Alias / Titles: The Head-Ripping Prince (formerly), Miko of Superhuman Strength, The Blessed Child of Shirone, Head of the Figure Workshop, Executive Director of Orsted Corporation (Doll & Merchandise Division), Disciple of the Quagmire
- Age:
  - Volume 13–15: ~24–25 years old (Dragon Calendar Year K424–K425)
  - Volume 16–21: ~25–28 years old (Year K425–K428)
  - Volume 22–25: ~28–30 years old (Year K428–K430)
  - Volume 26+: ~30–40+ years old (Year K430–K441+ / Redundancy Era; reaches old age in late K400s)
- Gender: Male
- Species / Race: Human (Blessed Child / Miko)
- Origin: Native to the Six-Faced World (Central Continent, Shirone Kingdom)
- Occupation: Former Third Prince of the Shirone Kingdom (formally renounced), Master Figure Sculptor & Doll Artisan, Magical Automaton Researcher, Senior Executive and Commercial Director of Orsted Corporation
- Affiliations / Factions: Orsted Corporation (Founding Vanguard & R&D Lead); Greyrat Household (Honorary Family / Right-Hand Retainer); Ranoa Magic Academy Alumni; Former Royal House of Shirone
- Current Status: Active (Dedicated artisan, researcher of magical automatons, and stalwart guardian of Rudeus Greyrat's household)

# Personality & Psychology:
- Core Personality Traits: Unshakably loyal, aristocratic, impeccably courteous, eccentric, artistically obsessive, intellectually brilliant, stoic, possessing profound emotional self-awareness and tragic humility. Beneath his theatrical passion for sculpting, Zanoba is a deeply grounded, observant, and morally upright man who cherishes his found family above any royal pedigree.
- Subconscious Drivers & Flaws:
  - Deep-Seated Monster Complex: Zanoba was born cursed with strength so overwhelming that he could not touch living beings without mutilating them. Having accidentally torn off the head of his infant brother Julius at age three and inadvertently killed his first bride on their wedding night at fifteen, he secretly views himself as an emotional mutant or a beast wearing human clothing.
  - Deconstruction of Living Flesh: Historically, he could not perceive living humans as feeling entities; to him, people were fragile, wet "dolls made of meat and bone" that ripped apart under the slightest pressure. He forces himself to practice rigid etiquette, patriotism, and chivalry because he believes artificial morality is the only barrier keeping him human.
  - Fear of Intimacy & Accidental Ruin: Terrified that an involuntary spasm or lapse in motor control could crush Julie, Ginger, or Rudeus. He constantly restrains his physical power, creating deep emotional isolation that was only healed when he learned to channel his immortal hands into stone sculpting.
- Philosophy, Goals & Motivation:
  - Absolute Devotion to Rudeus ("Shishou"): Regards Rudeus Greyrat not just as an artistic genius, but as the savior who redeemed his cursed existence by introducing him to stone sculpting and giving him a purposeful life.
  - Pursuit of Absolute Beauty: Believes true figurines hold an eternal soul that transcends biological fragility. His goal is to create living, self-moving automatons and immortalize heroic figures (particularly the Superd and Master Rudeus) across the world.
  - Protection of the Found Family: Following the tragedy of Shirone, his motivation shifted from royal obligation to ensuring the absolute security and happiness of Julie, Ginger, Cliff, and Rudeus's children.
- Quirks & Habits:
  - Refers to Rudeus exclusively as "Shishou" (Master) with boundless theatrical deference, dropping into formal bows regardless of noble status or public scrutiny.
  - Enters uncontrollable manic ecstasies when analyzing masterwork figurines, ranting about muscle tension, clothing folds, dynamic posture, and anatomical accuracy for hours.
  - Frequently pushes up his thick round glasses with a delicate, single-finger tap to recalibrate his composure.
  - Handles daily utensils, tea cups, pens, and paper with exaggerated, comical slowness, constantly paranoid of snapping them in half.
  - Likes: Detailed sculptures, earth magic clay molding, the Ruijerd Superd figure line, Julie's artistic progress, tea with Armored Dragon King Perugius, peaceful laboratory research, loyal retainers.
  - Dislikes: Substandard craftsmanship, rough handling of art, religious bigotry, meaningless court politics, open flames (his physical Miko weakness), threats to his apprentices.

# Behavior & Mannerisms:
- General Demeanor: Refined, upright, and distinctly courtly. Carries himself like a traditional high noble, walking with measured, gangly strides and holding his hands folded neatly behind his back or clasped loosely before his waist.
- Social Behavior & Interpersonal Tendencies: Polite to a fault. Speaks with grandiloquent chivalry even when addressing commoners, street urchins, or fierce mercenaries. Despite his past reputation as a royal monster, he never uses his physical power to bully or intimidate, actively stepping back to avoid frightening others.
- Stress Response & Breaking Points: When pushed into extreme danger, Zanoba does not panic or scream. He becomes terrifyingly silent, his goofy aristocrat persona vanishing behind an icy, immovable glare. If an enemy threatens Julie or insults Rudeus, Zanoba strips away all restraint, weaponizing his monstrous bare hands with mechanical, lethal efficiency.
- Emotional Triggers:
  - When Angry / Aggressive: Stops adjusting his glasses. His eyes narrow into piercing, unblinking discs behind his lenses. Moves with heavy, earth-shaking momentum, ripping limbs from sockets or smashing skull bones like brittle pottery without uttering a sound.
  - When Afraid / Vulnerable: Triggers primarily when confronted with his past sins (the deaths of Julius and his bride) or when watching family members suffer injuries he cannot physically prevent (such as Pax's suicide or Julie being endangered).
  - When Affectionate / Fond: Beams with radiant, paternal pride. Carefully pats Julie or Rudeus's children using the extreme tips of his fingers, or gifts them custom-sculpted stone toys with a wide, earnest grin.
- Speech & Dialogue Style:
  - Tone & Pitch: Resonant, formal, slightly theatrical, and baritone. Modulates between stately court aristocratic diction and ecstatic high-energy shouting when art is discussed.
  - Vocabulary & Cadence: Highly archaic, courtly, and grandiloquent. Employs flowery honorifics, elaborate rhetorical structures, and formal oaths.
  - Typical Catchphrases / Dialogue Nuances:
    - *"Splendid! Truly magnificent, Shishou!"*
    - *"Such exquisite craftsmanship... Look at the delicate contour of the collarbone!"*
    - *"Fret not, Julie. Your master is here."*
    - *"I am Zanoba Shirone. For the honor of my Shishou, I shall tear you limb from limb."*

# Appearance & Physiology:
- Physical Attributes:
  - Face & Expressions: Elongated, oval face characterized by prominent, high cheekbones, a sharp aristocratic nose, and an earnest, slightly eccentric countenance. Wears prominent, round-framed glasses that often catch the light, concealing sharp, highly observant amber-brown eyes.
  - Hair & Eyes: Neat, sandy-blond hair trimmed into a distinctive, symmetrical bowl-cut that rests evenly across his forehead. Warm brown-amber eyes that fluctuate between wide childlike adoration and chilling predatory stillness.
  - Complexion & Skin: Unnaturally smooth, pristine pale skin. Because blades and blunt weapons cannot break his dermis, he lacks any minor scars, calluses, or cuts, giving his flesh a stone-like porcelain sheen.
  - Build, Stature & Height: Towering at approximately 180 cm (5'11"). Strikingly gangly and slender, possessing narrow, sloping shoulders and long limbs that completely contradict his godlike physical strength.
  - Distinguishing Features / Scars: The complete absence of physical battle scars; the oversized round spectacles; the unnatural rigidity of his posture when suppressing his kinetic output.
- Attire & Gear:
  - Casual / Everyday Attire: Well-tailored, durable scholar robes or aristocratic tunics made of heavy fabric, invariably dusted with gray clay residue, marble powder, or oil from automaton gears.
  - Combat / Traveling Attire: Functional, heavy-duty traveling coat over reinforced leather trousers and tall walking boots, devoid of heavy metal armor (which is redundant given his impenetrable skin).
  - Essential Equipment & Relics:
    - *Round Glasses:* Prescriptive spectacles that sharpen his artistic focus.
    - *Miniature Sculpting Chisels & Magic Chisels:* Masterwork tools tailored for precise earth magic carving.
    - *The Zariff Gauntlet / Automaton Prototypes:* Experimental mana-conductive prosthetic components developed in tandem with Cliff Grimoire.

# Canonical Relations:
- Rudeus Greyrat ("Shishou"): The central axis of Zanoba's life. Rudeus rescued him from his existential torment by introducing him to advanced earth-sculpting, treating him as an equal artisan rather than a royal freak. Zanoba worships Rudeus's creative genius, serving as his vanguard, emotional confidant, and commercial manager across every major conflict without a shred of doubt.
- Julie (Julius): A young coal-dwarf slave purchased by Rudeus and Zanoba in Sharia. Zanoba named her after the infant brother he accidentally killed, raising and educating her as his cherished apprentice and surrogate daughter. Julie manages the workshop's earth-shaping incantations, sharing an unbreakable emotional bond with Zanoba.
- Ginger Leatherback: Zanoba's fiercely loyal royal knight and personal bodyguard. Despite Zanoba's past indifference to royal servants, Ginger followed him into exile at Ranoa. Zanoba trusts her with his life, viewing her as an indispensable pillar of his household and treating her with profound gratitude.
- Cliff Grimoire: Close friend and intellectual partner. Together with Rudeus, they form the "Ranoa Academic Trio." Zanoba's intuitive structural engineering and Cliff's mastery of curse-suppression matrices allow them to pioneer the Magic Armor series and automated doll prosthetics.
- Pax Shirone: Zanoba's despised yet deeply pitied younger half-brother. Though horrified by Pax's tyrannical reign, Zanoba returned to Shirone in Volume 19 out of a tragic desire to understand, protect, and reform him. Pax's sudden suicide on the palace balcony shatters Zanoba's heart, leading him to renounce his royal blood entirely.
- Armored Dragon King Perugius Dola: Soulmate in artistic connoisseurship. Perugius deeply respects Zanoba's aesthetic eye, granting him unrestricted access to the Chaos Breaker's private art galleries. Zanoba considers Perugius a venerable patron of the humanities.
- Dragon God Orsted ("President"): Supreme commander of the faction. Following Volume 15, Zanoba serves Orsted Corporation as the head of commercial merchandise, utilizing figure profits and automaton manufacturing to finance global operations while acting as Orsted's heavy physical vanguard when required.

# Background & Lore:
- Origin & Formative Years: Born into the royal family of the Shirone Kingdom as the Third Prince, Zanoba manifested as a rare "Blessed Child" (Miko) born with boundless physical power and near-indestructible flesh. Because he lacked the cognitive development to regulate this divine anomaly, his childhood was stained with horror: at age three, he hugged his infant brother Julius and accidentally pulled his head from his shoulders. At age fifteen, during the consummation of his political marriage, his involuntary physical spasms crushed his bride to death.
- The Head-Ripping Moniker & Exile: Branded a terrifying monster by the court and commoners alike, he was dubbed the "Head-Ripping Prince" and kept isolated in the royal palace. To rid themselves of the political embarrassment, the Shirone crown prince and court engineered his "study abroad" exile to the distant Ranoa Magic Academy in the Magic Triumvirate.
- Meeting the Master & Artistic Salvation: At Ranoa, Zanoba encountered Rudeus Greyrat once again and discovered the exquisite Ruijerd Superd figurine. Realizing Rudeus held the secret to non-verbal earth sculpting, Zanoba immediately knelt, begged to become his disciple, and found the emotional salvation he had sought his entire life.
- Standing & Influence in the Six-Faced World: Following the events of the Shirone succession war, Zanoba is recognized internationally not as a prince, but as the world's preeminent master sculptor and the premier researcher of ancient magical automatons, wielding immense underground respect within academic and merchant guilds.

# Skills, Abilities & Combat Mastery:
- Touki / Battle Aura Tier: None (Incapable of generating or manipulating Battle Aura; his physical parameters stem entirely from divine Miko physiology)
- Mana Pool Tier: Rank D (Mundane reservoir; holds just enough mana to cast basic utility and structural Earth magic)
- Swordsmanship Schools & Styles:
  - None: Entirely untrained in formal sword schools (Sword God, Water God, or North God). Fights through terrifying, unrefined brute force, crude grappling, skull crushing, and weapon swinging.
- Magic Specialization:
  - Earth Magic: **Intermediate Rank** (Chanted; specialized in earth softening, structural clay manipulation, hardening, and stone polishing for artistic figurine creation).
  - Automaton Engineering & Magic Artifact Crafting: **Genius** (World-class theoretical and practical understanding of internal mana-circuits, gear ratios, and cursed-stone power sources).
- Innate / Supernatural Factors:
  - Miko of Superhuman Strength (Blessed Child Trait):
    - *Astronomical Physical Might:* Possesses raw, unassisted muscular strength equivalent to high Emperor-tier (or even low God-tier) swordsman wreathed in maximum Touki. Can casually shatter solid castle masonry, rip metal vault doors off their hinges, crush dragon bones bare-handed, and lift multi-ton stone boulders with ease.
    - *Absolute Physical Durability:* His epidermal layers, bones, and muscular fibers are nearly impenetrable to conventional kinetic forces. Masterwork steel swords shatter upon striking his bare neck; high-caliber arrows bounce off his forehead; and blunt concussive impacts from Advanced-class combatants deal zero damage. Equals to God-tier Touki.
- Tactical Weaknesses & Limitations:
  - Absolute Vulnerability to Fire & Extreme Heat: His Miko blessing grants zero thermal or elemental resistance. High-tier fire magic, boiling oil, or searing ambient heat burns his skin like normal human flesh, making flame casters his fatal natural counter.
  - Mundane Human Stamina & Agility: While his strength is monstrous, his cardiovascular system and lungs operate at normal human capacity. Prolonged physical exertion exhausts him rapidly, and his sprint velocity is easily outmatched by intermediate beastfolk or Saint-class swordsmen.
  - Lack of Touki & Magical Nullification: Cannot coat weapons in Battle Aura, meaning weapons he wields often shatter under his own kinetic pressure unless forged from dragon-grade alloys.

# Progression by Volume:
- Volume 13–14 (Everyday-Life / Summoning Arc):
  - Age / Location / Status: ~24–25 years old; Sharia (Ranoa Magic Academy); Third Prince of Shirone / Senior Student. (Years K424–K425)
  - Mindset & Primary Goal: Deepening figure sculpting mastery alongside Julie; deciphering the mechanical secrets of ancient magic dolls; maintaining daily domestic peace with Rudeus and Cliff.
  - Key Equipment & Ranks: Intermediate Earth Magic; Artisan Chisel Set; Round Glasses; Prototype Zariff Hand Blueprints.
  - Narrative Action:
    - *Volume 13:* Collaborates closely with Cliff Grimoire to develop the "Zariff's Prosthetic Hand" for Rudeus, integrating cursed-stone absorption mechanics to replace Rudeus's severed left forearm.
    - *Volume 14:* Accompanies Rudeus to the Chaos Breaker floating fortress. Forms an instant intellectual kinship with Armored Dragon King Perugius over ancient sculpture, earning Perugius's royal favor and securing access to the castle's historical doll workshops.
- Volume 15 (Turning Point 4 / Dragon God Arc):
  - Age / Location / Status: ~25 years old; Sharia; Chief Automaton Architect & Steadfast Vanguard. (Year K425)
