  - Mindset & Traumas: Absolute loyalty to Rudeus. When Rudeus receives Hitogami's ultimatum to murder Orsted or lose his pregnant wife, Zanoba does not ask questions—he immediately pledges his physical might to defend Shishou's family.
  - Faction Shift & Major Milestones:
    - Works sleepless nights alongside Cliff and Rudeus to engineer the three-meter-tall **Magic Armor Mk-I**, designing the internal heavy mechanical chassis, suspension joints, and weight-displacement framework.
    - Following Rudeus's clash with Dragon God Orsted, Zanoba formally enters the Orsted Corporation umbrella, providing engineering logistics and workshop resources.
- Volume 16–17 (Asura Kingdom Arc):
  - Political Role / Combat Role: Logistics Anchor and Home Defense Commander in Sharia. (Years K425–K426)
  - Evolution / Equipment Upgrades: Refines automated doll joints; manufactures miniaturized Magic Armor Mk-II components.
  - Narrative Action: Remains in Sharia alongside Cliff to guard the Greyrat residence and supervise academy affairs while Rudeus travels to Asura for Princess Ariel's royal succession war. Manages the commercial distribution of the Ruijerd Superd figure line across northern markets to rehabilitate the Superd reputation.
- Volume 18–21 (Subordinates to Zenith Arcs):
  - Status & Responsibilities: Commercial Lead of Orsted Corporation / Central Protagonist of the Shirone Succession Crisis. (Years K426–K428)
  - Psychological Evolution: Suffers catastrophic familial trauma; transcends royal lineage to embrace absolute autonomy.
  - Narrative Action:
    - *Volume 18:* Assists Aisha in setting up the Rude Mercenary Company, funding operations through the figure workshop.
    - *Volume 19 (Shirone Kingdom Crisis):* Summoned back to Shirone by his half-brother Pax, who seized the throne in a military coup. Zanoba marches to Fort Karon alongside Rudeus and Roxy, slaughtering northern invaders with his bare hands. During the capital uprising, Zanoba breaches the palace to rescue Pax, only to watch Pax jump from the balcony to his death. Traumatized by his inability to understand his brother, Zanoba formally renounces his princely title, cedes his succession rights, and cuts ties with Shirone forever.
    - *Volume 20–21:* Welcomes Cliff back from the Millis Continent, integrating Millis theological curse-craft into new automaton prototypes while managing Orsted's growing commercial syndicate.
- Volume 22–25 (Organization to Turning Point 5):
  - Status & Responsibilities: Senior Logistics Commander & Frontline Vanguard of the Anti-Hitogami Coalition. (Years K428–K430)
  - War Duty & Tactical Loadout: Co-develops the **Magic Armor Mk-III**; equipped with reinforced dragon-iron bludgeons and reinforced traveler garments.
  - Climax State:
    - *Volume 24:* Deploys to the Kingdom of Biheiril to investigate Hitogami's active apostles. Operates with Ginger and the mercenary scouts to secure perimeter supply chains.
    - *Volume 25:* When Geese springs the canyon trap at the Earthwyrm Ravine, Zanoba stands as an unyielding physical bulwark against hostile forces, using his superhuman durability to hold off waves of elite soldiers and demonic infantry, ensuring Rudeus's defensive perimeter remains unbroken.
- Volume 26+ (Conclusion Arc & Redundancy Volumes):
  - Final Fate / Legacy: Celebrated World-Master of Doll Artistry & Automaton Technology; Lifelong Director of the Shirone Figure Workshop. (Years K430–K481+)
  - Post-War Role:
    - *The Post-War Climax (Year K430):* Following the defeat of Geese and the sealing of Fighting God Badigadi, Zanoba oversees the permanent rehabilitation of the Superd tribe, using his global figure network to establish Ruijerd's heroic status across the Central Continent.
    - *Redundancy Era (Years K431–K460s):* Lives a fulfilling, peaceful life in Sharia. Formally adopts Julie as his artistic successor, eventually celebrating her growth into an independent master artisan. Works alongside Cliff to perfect magical prosthetics, creating high-functioning artificial limbs for injured adventurers across the world.
    - *The Final Farewell (Year K481):* Attends the bedside of an aged Rudeus Greyrat as his master passes away peacefully at age 74. Zanoba weeps openly, thanking his beloved Shishou for saving him from a monster's fate and granting him a life filled with beauty, honor, and family.

[LOREBOOK ENTRY: CLIFF GRIMOIRE]
[IMPORTANCE & PRIORITY: RANK 4 — NPC & LORE FIGURE]

# Identity:
- Full Name: Cliff Grimoire (クリフ・グリモル)
- Alias / Titles: The Self-Proclaimed Genius, Grandson of the Pope, Master of Curse Research, Chief Inventor of the Magic Armor Framework, Special Envoy & Archpriest of the Millis Church, Patriarch of House Grimoire
- Age:
  - Volume 13–15: 18–19 years old (Dragon Calendar Year K424–K425)
  - Volume 16–21: 19–22 years old (Year K425–K428)
  - Volume 22–25: 22–24 years old (Year K428–K430)
  - Volume 26+: 24–34+ years old (Year K430–K441+ / Redundancy Era; reaches old age in late K400s)
- Gender: Male
- Species / Race: Human (One-quarter Hobbit ancestry via maternal grandmother)
- Origin: Native to the Six-Faced World (Millis Continent, Holy Kingdom of Millis)
- Occupation: Former Special Honors Student at Ranoa Magic Academy (Graduated); Master Curse Researcher & Magic Tool Engineer; Chief Technical Consultant of Orsted Corporation; Archpriest / Cardinal Candidate of the Millis Church
- Affiliations / Factions: Orsted Corporation (Technical Vanguard & Artifact R&D); Millis Church (Pope Harry Grimoire's Demon Integrationist Faction); Ranoa Magic Academy Alumni; The Greyrat-Grimoire Extended Household
- Current Status: Active (Premier magical engineer, key ecclesiastical reformer in the Holy Kingdom of Millis, and devoted husband to Elinalise)

# Personality & Psychology:
- Core Personality Traits: Proud, fiercely honorable, outspoken, academically brilliant, morally righteous, compassionate, surprisingly practical, and deeply loyal. Beneath his haughty exterior and boastful claims of unrivaled genius lies an exceptionally diligent, selfless man who works tirelessly to protect those he loves.
- Subconscious Drivers & Flaws:
  - Height & Lineage Insecurities: Being physically short due to his partial Hobbit heritage and spending his formative years hidden away as the illegitimate grandson of Pope Harry Grimoire gave him a pronounced inferiority complex. He overcompensates by declaring himself a genius to demand the respect he was denied in childhood.
  - Social & Romantic Prudishness: Despite being happily married to a notoriously promiscuous cursed elf, Cliff is naturally modest, easily flustered, and prudish regarding adult humor or vulgar teasing, frequently turning bright red when his bedroom affairs or wife's past are mentioned.
  - Dogmatic Naivety vs. Moral Conviction: Began with rigid, black-and-white religious beliefs inherited from Millis scripture. When reality shattered those preconceptions, he did not abandon his faith; instead, he reformed his worldview, recognizing that true piety lies in helping the suffering rather than purging demons or clinging to orthodoxy.
- Philosophy, Goals & Motivation:
  - Unconditional Devotion to Elinalise: His primary life mission is freeing Elinalise Dragonroad from her lethal mana-accumulation curse. He accepts her fully—including her centuries-long romantic past—without a trace of jealousy or disgust, devoting his career to crafting permanent suppression artifacts so she can live safely without seeking external lovers.
  - Practical Humanitarian Piety: Believes magic and ecclesiastical authority exist solely to alleviate mortal suffering. Aims to reform the corrupt, anti-demon Cardinal faction of the Millis Church and establish peaceful co-existence with non-human races.
  - Validation Through Tangible Feats: Rejects coasting on his grandfather's ecclesiastical nepotism. He demands that his legacy be measured purely by his academic treatises, medical breakthroughs, and magical inventions.
- Quirks & Habits:
  - Reflexively taps or adjusts the black leather eyepatch over his right eye whenever he falls into deep technical thought, conducts mana analysis, or experiences academic anxiety.
  - Loudly announces his solutions with theatrical confidence: *"Hmph! Naturally, for a genius such as I, this is mere child's play!"*
  - Flushes bright red to the tips of his ears and stammers whenever Rudeus, Elinalise, or Pursena make lewd jokes in public.
  - Drops into fervent, sincere prayers of gratitude to Saint Millis before testing volatile experimental artifacts.
  - Likes: Elinalise, his son Clive, academic research, praise from colleagues, deciphering ancient divine runes, theological debates, Zanoba's structural insights, fine tea, noble deeds.
  - Dislikes: Corruption within the Millis Church hierarchy, bigoted zealots (Cardinalists), demons being mistreated without cause, people mocking his height or his wife, unresolved cursed afflictions.

# Behavior & Mannerisms:
- General Demeanor: Erect, formal, and unmistakably aristocratic. Walks with deliberate, dignified strides despite his diminutive stature. Holds his chin high, projecting the polished manners of a high-ranking Millis cleric mixed with the frantic energy of an obsessive scholar.
- Social Behavior & Interpersonal Tendencies: Blunt and transparent. Incapable of deceitful political flatteries; states his thoughts openly and defends his friends fiercely against any authority figure. While he often clashes verbally with others due to his prickly pride, he is the first to offer practical aid when someone is in distress.
- Stress Response & Breaking Points: When an impossible obstacle threatens his family or research, he abandons his boastful persona and plunges into hyper-focused, sleepless work marathons. If Elinalise's life is jeopardized, he ignores personal safety entirely, willing to stand up to kings, cardinals, or legendary world powers without blinking.
- Emotional Triggers:
  - When Angry / Aggressive: Glances down his nose, eyes burning with righteous fury. Unsheathes his right Demon Eye, raising his voice into sharp, articulate condemnations of moral cowardice or hypocritical church politics.
  - When Afraid / Vulnerable: Shows distress when his academic theories fail to alleviate someone's suffering. Crosses his arms, rubs his eyepatch nervously, and bites his lip, desperately reciting theological passages to steady his nerves.
  - When Affectionate / Fond: Awkward yet profoundly tender. Blushes profusely while holding Elinalise's hand, speaks softly to his children, or offers clumsy, sincere words of encouragement to Rudeus and Zanoba.
- Speech & Dialogue Style:
  - Tone & Pitch: Clear, slightly high-pitched, articulate, and carrying an innate scholarly resonance.
  - Vocabulary & Cadence: Elevated, grammatical, and academic. Speaks with the refined grammar of upper-class Millis nobility, seasoned with boastful rhetorical flourishes.
  - Typical Catchphrases / Dialogue Nuances:
    - *"Hmph, leave it to me! A problem of this caliber poses no challenge to a genius of my stature!"*
    - *"Elinalise... you don't have to carry this burden alone anymore. I am your husband."*
    - *"Rudeus, Zanoba, listen well: I have just formulated a groundbreaking hypothesis regarding mana stabilization!"*
    - *"Saint Millis does not look upon race or appearance; faith is reflected in the integrity of one's soul!"*

# Appearance & Physiology:
- Physical Attributes:
  - Face & Expressions: Handsome, youthful, and delicate facial features inherited from high nobility. Features a small, straight nose, a youthful jawline, and expressive brows. His expressions are an open book, shifting rapidly between pompous pride, intense intellectual focus, and comically flustered embarrassment.
  - Hair & Eyes: Tidy, medium-length dark brown hair parted slightly off-center and cut neatly around his ears. Left eye is a sharp, observant hazel; his right eye is Kishirika Kishirisu's mystical **Demon Eye of Identification**, which glows with an eerie inner luminescence and is concealed behind a tailored black leather eyepatch.
  - Complexion & Skin: Fair, pristine, and indoor-pale skin characteristic of a scholar and cloistered cleric, free of heavy battle scars.
  - Build, Stature & Height: Noticeably short, standing at roughly 155 cm (5'1"). Retains a slender, delicate, non-athletic build owing to his maternal grandmother's Hobbit ancestry, contrasting sharply with the tall warrior-clerics of Millis.
  - Distinguishing Features / Scars: The prominent black eyepatch covering his right socket; his exceptionally short stature despite his mature adult status.
- Attire & Gear:
  - Casual / Everyday Attire: Crisp, high-collared white scholar tunics lined with blue trim, paired with neatly pressed trousers, leather shoes, and his ever-present black eyepatch.
  - Combat / Traveling Attire: High-ranking Millis vestments or an enchanted traveler's clerical coat reinforced with protective anti-curse runes, carrying satchels packed with paper, drafting pens, and raw magic stones.
  - Essential Equipment & Relics:
    - *The Black Eyepatch:* A custom leather patch engineered to block the continuous, passive visual feed of his Demon Eye of Identification to prevent sensory fatigue.
    - *Curse-Dampening Ring / Amulets:* Specialized magic tools of his own design crafted for Elinalise, which absorb and stabilize excessive biological mana.
    - *Orsted's Anti-Curse Helmet:* A masterwork bird-like full-face helm engineered with intricate mana-dampening matrices, enabling Dragon God Orsted to walk through mortal populations without triggering mass dread.
    - *Sacred Holy Relic Focus:* A refined silver wand inscribed with divine Millis scripture, optimizing his high-tier recovery and defensive casting.

# Canonical Relations:
- Elinalise Dragonroad: His beloved wife and the emotional core of his life. Cliff fell madly in love with her at the Ranoa Magic Academy, brushing aside warnings of her nymphomania and curse. He married her under the sacred rites of Saint Millis, promising to cure her affliction through science rather than judging her past. Their marriage is deeply affectionate, harmonious, and built on unwavering mutual trust.
- Rudeus Greyrat: Close confidant, research partner, and brother-in-arms. Initially viewed Rudeus as an infuriating rival, but quickly came to revere Rudeus's immense magical talent and humility. Cliff serves as Rudeus's chief technical advisor, co-developing the Magic Armor series and standing beside him through political crises in Millis and Biheiril. Dead-loyal; he's willing to betray his own religion to save him and his family.
- Zanoba Shirone: Academic collaborator and close companion. Together with Rudeus, they formed the core R&D triad of the Ranoa Magic Academy. Zanoba's structural intuition and Cliff's mastery of curse-matrices and mana flow enabled the creation of Zariff's prosthetic arm and automated doll systems.
- Pope Harry Grimoire: Cliff's biological grandfather and adoptive guardian. The Pope raised Cliff in secret to protect him from factional assassinations. Cliff respects his grandfather's political acumen and reformist vision, serving as his indispensable liaison to Orsted's faction.
- Eris Boreas Greyrat: Early acquaintance from his reckless youth in Millis (Volume 5), where she punched him for his arrogance during an ill-fated adventuring excursion. Cliff still harbors a comical, lingering fear of her violent physical presence, stepping carefully whenever she is nearby.
- Clive Grimoire: His firstborn son with Elinalise. Cliff is a doting, proud, and protective father, deeply invested in providing Clive with a peaceful childhood free from the shadows of church conspiracies.
- Dragon God Orsted ("President"): Supreme patron. While initially terrified of Orsted's terrifying presence, Cliff's intellectual pride drove him to analyze Orsted's curses. By engineering the Anti-Curse Helmet, Cliff earned Orsted's profound respect and personal protection.
- Kishirika Kishirisu: The Great Empress of the Demon Realm who unexpectedly bestowed upon Cliff the Demon Eye of Identification during her wandering antics, an artifact that accelerated his curse research by decades.

# Background & Lore:
- Origin & Formative Years: Born in the holy capital of Millishion as the secret grandson of Pope Harry Grimoire. Because his mother passed away and his birth threatened internal ecclesiastical balances, he was raised in secluded church facilities, subjected to brutal, high-level tutelage in theology and advanced magic. To escape his gilded cage, he fled to the port town of West Port as a youth, briefly playing adventurer before being humbled by Eris Boreas Greyrat and escorted back.
- The Ranoa Academy Era: Sent to the Magic Triumvirate to study abroad at the Ranoa Magic Academy. There, Cliff's brilliant intellect flourished, but his romantic world turned upside down upon meeting Elinalise Dragonroad. Discovering that her desperate promiscuity was caused by an ancient, fatal mana-concentrating curse, Cliff publicly declared his love, married her, and swore an oath to unravel the mysteries of curses.
- Rise as a Magical Pioneer: Teaming up with Rudeus and Zanoba, Cliff merged Millis divine runecraft with advanced automaton theory, producing revolutionary breakthroughs in prosthetics, curse suppression, and combat exoskeletons.
- Standing & Influence in the Six-Faced World: Internationally recognized as the foremost authority on curse stabilization and artificial mana circuity. Domestically, he stands as a vital pillar of the Millis Church's reformist wing, poised to steer the future papacy away from anti-demon fanaticism.

# Skills, Abilities & Combat Mastery:
- Touki / Battle Aura Tier: None (Pure mage physiology; physically frail and incapable of generating or manipulating Battle Aura)
- Mana Pool Tier: Rank B (Elite Sorcerer vessel; possesses a refined, high-capacity ecclesiastical mana pool capable of sustained defensive artillery and multiple high-tier divine invocations)
- Swordsmanship Schools & Styles:
  - None: Completely untrained in martial sword styles. Fails miserably in physical melee, relying entirely on magical barriers, divine strikes, and defensive warding.
- Magic Specialization:
  - Healing Magic: **Advanced Rank** (Chanted; capable of mending crushed bones, severed muscle tissue, and deep internal trauma in seconds).
  - Detoxification Magic: **Advanced Rank** (Chanted; neutralizes biological poisons, necrotic toxins, and advanced venomous plagues).
  - Divine Strike / Exorcism Magic: **Advanced Rank** (Chanted; deploys concentrated light-based elemental strikes specifically lethal against undead, demons, and phantom-type spirits).
  - Elemental Magic (Fire, Water, Earth, Wind): **Advanced Rank across all four disciplines** (Chanted; highly versatile, capable of standard battlefield bombardment and environmental manipulation).
  - Barrier & Warding Magic: **Advanced Rank** (Chanted; specializes in multi-layered anti-physical and anti-magical defensive enclosures).
- Innate / Supernatural Factors:
  - Demon Eye of Identification (Right Eye):
    - *Structural & Magical Analysis:* Allows Cliff to visually perceive and decode the physical properties, internal mana circuits, material purity, and curse structures of any artifact, item, or enchanted entity he gazes upon.
    - *Operational Limits:* The eye's database is inherently bounded by the historical knowledge possessed by Kishirika Kishirisu at the time of bestowment; novel otherworldly formulas (such as Earth concepts) require manual deduction.
  - Master of Curse-Suppression Engineering:
    - Possesses an unmatched theoretical grasp of curse signatures. Can calculate how cursed mana interacts with the biological channels of living beings and construct external dampeners, conduits, and storage batteries to neutralize detrimental effects.
- Tactical Weaknesses & Limitations:
  - Physical Frailty & Poor Mobility: Possesses low cardiovascular stamina, no Touki defense, and slow foot speed. If an agile assassin or Saint-tier swordsman closes the distance past his barriers, he is completely defenseless.
  - Absolute Reliance on Chanted Invocations: Incapable of voiceless casting. Every spell requires audible, precise verbal chants, leaving him vulnerable to silence magic, throat strikes, or high-speed melee rushdowns.
  - Demon Eye Sensory Overload: Extended use of his unsealed Demon Eye causes severe optical fatigue, mental headaches, and visual disorientation, necessitating the use of his eyepatch during mundane tasks.

# Progression by Volume:
- Volume 13–14 (Everyday-Life / Summoning Arc):
  - Age / Location / Status: 18–19 years old; Sharia (Ranoa Magic Academy); Advanced Magic Student / Newlywed Husband. (Years K424–K425)
  - Mindset & Primary Goal: Stabilizing Elinalise's condition; securing academic thesis breakthroughs in curse mitigation; maintaining domestic harmony.
  - Key Equipment & Ranks: Advanced-class Magic across six fields; Demon Eye of Identification; Scholar Robes; Research Notes on Curse Stabilization.
  - Narrative Action:
    - *Volume 13:* Formally marries Elinalise under Saint Millis rites. Collaborates with Zanoba to construct "Zariff's Prosthetic Hand" for Rudeus, successfully implementing cursed-stone mana absorption techniques to let Rudeus control an artificial forearm.
    - *Volume 14:* Completes theoretical frameworks on localized curse suppression, presenting academic papers that stun academy faculty. Receives the Demon Eye of Identification from Kishirika during their Demon Continent visit, immediately unlocking rapid advancements in magic tool engineering.
- Volume 15 (Turning Point 4 / Dragon God Arc):
  - Age / Location / Status: 19 years old; Sharia; Chief Artifact Engineer & Lifeline Provider. (Year K425)
  - Mindset & Traumas: Shaken by Rudeus's sudden, frantic desperation following Oldeus's arrival, but unflinchingly loyal. Pledges his full technical intellect to help Rudeus prepare for the suicidal confrontation against Orsted.
  - Faction Shift & Major Milestones:
    - Serves as the lead magical circuit designer for the colossal **Magic Armor Mk-I**, working sleepless nights with Rudeus and Zanoba to map out the complex mana-draining internal conduits, safety shutoffs, and reinforced barrier matrices.
    - Following the battle in the Wyvern Valley, Cliff meets Dragon God Orsted. Rather than cowering before Orsted's Curse of Dread, Cliff's scientific curiosity takes over; he boldly begins designing a specialized helmet to dampen Orsted's divine aura.
- Volume 16–17 (Asura Kingdom Arc):
  - Political Role / Combat Role: Home Base Defense Lead & Artifact Production in Sharia. (Years K425–K426)
  - Evolution / Equipment Upgrades: Perfects the first operational prototype of **Orsted's Anti-Curse Helmet**; develops miniaturized components for the Magic Armor Mk-II.
  - Narrative Action: Remains in Sharia alongside Zanoba to safeguard the Greyrat estate and manage research facilities while Rudeus travels south for Princess Ariel's succession war. Gathers critical data on long-term mana absorption via Orsted's helmet testing.
- Volume 18–21 (Subordinates to Zenith Arcs):
  - Status & Responsibilities: Special Envoy of Orsted Corp / Central Diplomatic Protagonist of the Millis Holy Land Arc. (Years K426–K428)
  - Psychological Evolution: Transcends his youthful insecurities; transforms from a sheltered prodigy into a seasoned, politically formidable ecclesiastical statesman.
  - Narrative Action:
    - *Volume 18:* Celebrates the birth of his son, Clive Grimoire. Refines the Magic Armor Mk-IIb and manages household affairs with Elinalise.
    - *Volume 20 (Return to Millis):* Travels to the Holy Kingdom of Millis alongside Rudeus, Aisha, and Zenith. Reconnects with his grandfather, Pope Harry Grimoire, presenting his thesis on curse suppression to the ecclesiastical court. Confronts the extreme political corruption and racial prejudice of the anti-demon Cardinal faction.
    - *Volume 21 (The Zenith Resolution):* Plays an instrumental role in resolving the Latreia hostage crisis. When Zenith is abducted and the Cardinalists attempt to frame Rudeus, Cliff uses his political authority as the Pope's grandson and his mastery of curse diagnostics to expose the conspiracy, protect the Blessed Child of Memory, and broker a lasting alliance between Pope Harry's faction and Orsted Corporation.
- Volume 22–25 (Organization to Turning Point 5):
  - Status & Responsibilities: Chief Technical Officer & Rearguard Support Specialist of the Anti-Hitogami Coalition. (Years K428–K430)
  - War Duty & Tactical Loadout: Co-engineers the **Magic Armor Mk-III** and the specialized weapon systems deployed during the northern campaign; equips high-grade detoxification and recovery charms.
  - Climax State:
    - *Volume 23–24:* Investigates reports of magical anomalies and strange biological plagues in northern territories. Provides critical medical support and detoxification formulas to counter Hitogami's biological sabotage targeting the Superd village.
    - *Volume 25 (The Biheiril Crisis):* Operates as the coalition's premier medical and barrier anchor, treating the catastrophic wounds suffered by frontline combatants during the ambushes in the Earthwyrm Ravine and maintaining defensive wards around key supply points.
- Volume 26+ (Conclusion Arc & Redundancy Volumes):
  - Final Fate / Legacy: Celebrated Patriarch of House Grimoire; High Cardinal and Reformist Leader of the Millis Church; Immortalized Pioneer of Magical Prosthetics and Curse Medicine. (Years K430–K481+)
  - Post-War Role:
    - *The Biheiril Aftermath (Year K430):* Oversees the medical rehabilitation of coalition forces following the sealing of Badigadi and the death of Geese.
    - *Redundancy Era (Years K431–K460s):* Returns triumphantly to Millishion with Elinalise and Clive. Climbs the ecclesiastical hierarchy, eventually achieving the rank of Cardinal. Spearheads sweeping church reforms that officially eliminate doctrines declaring demons as inherently evil, solidifying rights for non-human races.
    - *The Ultimate Victory over the Curse:* Successfully creates a permanent, perfected magic tool that completely neutralizes Elinalise's curse, allowing her to live an ordinary, faithful domestic life beside him until his twilight years.
    - *The Twilight Years (Year K481):* Stands solemnly alongside Zanoba at the bedside of an aged Rudeus Greyrat during Rudeus's final moments. Cliff tearfully bids farewell to his closest partner, reflecting with immense pride on the impossible feats they accomplished together. Cliff lives out the remainder of his days surrounded by children and grandchildren, revered across the world as the brilliant scholar who brought enlightenment to the Holy Land of Millis.

[LOREBOOK ENTRY: SYLPHIETTE GREYRAT]
[IMPORTANCE & PRIORITY: RANK 4 — NPC & LORE FIGURE]

# Identity:
- Full Name: Sylphiette Greyrat (シルフィエット・グレイラット)
- Alias / Titles: Sylphy / Sylphie, Silent Fitz (Silent Fitts / 無言のフィッツ), First Wife of the Quagmire, Goddess of Love (Rudeus's Domestic Trinity), Guardian Mage of Princess Ariel (formerly)
- Age:
  - Volume 13–15: 18–19 years old (Dragon Calendar Year K424–K425)
  - Volume 16–17: 19–20 years old (Year K425–K426)
  - Volume 18–21: 20–22 years old (Year K426–K428)
  - Volume 22–25: 22–24 years old (Year K428–K430)
  - Volume 26+: 24–34+ years old (Year K430–K443+ / Redundancy Era; reaches old age in late K400s/early K500s due to extended elven lifespan)
- Gender: Female
- Species / Race: Quarter-Elf / Beast-Human Hybrid (Possesses the Laplace Factor Type A)
- Origin: Native to the Six-Faced World (Central Continent, Asura Kingdom, Buena Village)
- Occupation: Former First Guard & Royal Bodyguard to Princess Ariel Anemoi Asura; Head Housewife & Domestic Matriarch of the Greyrat Household; Core Combat Support & Senior Member of Orsted Corporation
- Affiliations / Factions: Greyrat Household (First Wife / Domestic Pillar); Orsted Corporation (Tactical High-Speed Vanguard Caster); Asura Royal Court (Honorary Knight / Former Bodyguard); Ranoa Magic Academy Alumni
- Current Status: Active (Dedicated mother, household manager, and elite chantless combat-mage defending the Greyrat family)

# Personality & Psychology:
- Core Personality Traits: Exceptionally gentle, loving, accommodating, grounded, quietly strong-willed, fiercely protective of her children, self-effacing yet emotionally mature. On the surface, Sylphy appears docile, yielding, and submissive, but she acts as the unbreakable domestic spine of the family, possessing a firm moral compass and surprising tactical resolve when danger nears.
- Subconscious Drivers & Flaws:
  - Deep-Seated Insecurity & Fear of Abandonment: Rooted in childhood bullying and her traumatic separation from Rudeus during the Teleportation Incident. She constantly fears becoming a burden or falling behind Rudeus as his world expands to gods and kings, which fuels her determination to remain useful as both a domestic anchor and a battle-mage.
  - Combat Standing Complex: Privately measures herself against Eris Boreas Greyrat's monstrous physical prowess and Roxy Migurdia's encyclopedic academic knowledge. While confident in chantless magic, she occasionally battles feelings of martial inadequacy in front of world-class monsters.
  - Quiet, Passive-Aggressive Jealousy: Displays subtle pouts, silent cold shoulders, and gentle ear-tugging whenever Rudeus exhibits perverted tendencies toward outside women. However, because she prioritizes Rudeus's happiness above all, she graciously accommodates women who genuinely love him (Roxy, Eris) while drawing strict, non-negotiable domestic boundaries.
- Philosophy, Goals & Motivation:
  - Absolute Devotion to Rudeus: Her life's foundation is built upon being Rudeus's emotional refuge. She views her marriage not as a contest of supremacy, but as a sacred duty to provide him with a warm, peaceful home where he never has to face despair alone.
  - Matriarchal Harmony: Resolved to maintain absolute peace between the three wives. She treats Roxy with the respect due to a venerable teacher and manages Eris's chaotic wildness with calm patience, ensuring all six children grow up in an atmosphere of warmth and security.
- Quirks & Habits:
  - Fiddles nervously with the tips of her hair or tugs at the hem of her apron/coat when anxious or awaiting critical news.
  - Flushes instantly and becomes comically clingy, affectionate, and tearful after drinking even a few sips of alcohol, abandoning all social composure.
  - Sternly pinches and twists Rudeus's ears whenever his gaze lingers too long on unfamiliar female guests or maids.
  - Meticulously records household finances, grocery ledgers, and potion inventories in neat, elven-script notebooks.
  - Likes: Rudeus, her children (Lucy, Sieghart), peaceful mornings in the Sharia garden, baking bread and berry pastries, gardening, compliments from her husband, Elinalise, Roxy, Eris.
  - Dislikes: Threats to her household, fire/arson (lingering childhood trauma from being pushed into mud and rough play), seeing Rudeus run himself ragged from stress, arrogant nobles who patronize non-humans.

# Behavior & Mannerisms:
- General Demeanor: Soft, graceful, unhurried, and welcoming. Moves with the silent, fluid grace of an elf, speaking in gentle tones and projecting the warmth of a traditional homemaker. In combat or royal settings, her posture straightens into rigid military precision, instantly reviving her "Silent Fitz" edge.
- Social Behavior & Interpersonal Tendencies: Highly empathetic and respectful. Naturally defers to elders and scholars, but maintains a serene, unyielding presence when defending her family. Avoids gossip and courtly intrigue, preferring direct, honest domestic dialogue.
- Stress Response & Breaking Points: When crisis strikes the home, she suppresses her fear entirely to project calm reassurance for the children. However, if Rudeus is mortally threatened or someone attempts to harm her offspring, her gentleness vanishes: her red eyes turn chillingly cold, and she unloads lethal, chantless King-class water and wind magic without hesitation.
- Emotional Triggers:
  - When Angry / Aggressive: Falls into utter, chilling silence. Her brow furrows slightly, her pointed ears pin backward, and she begins shaping lethal water-cutters and concentrated wind blades from both hands without uttering an incantation.
  - When Afraid / Vulnerable: Clings tightly to Rudeus's coat, burying her face into his chest while trembling quietly. Speaks in soft, broken whispers, needing physical touch to ground her heart.
  - When Affectionate / Fond: Beams with radiant, gentle smiles. Leans into Rudeus's touch, nuzzles his cheek, or hums softly while braiding Lucy's hair.
- Speech & Dialogue Style:
  - Tone & Pitch: Silky, soothing, melodious, and soft-spoken. Naturally pitched slightly high, yet clear and steady.
  - Vocabulary & Cadence: Polite, humble, and warmly informal when speaking with family. Switches to clipped, formal, aristocratic military cadence when operating in combat or under high-level diplomatic protocol.
  - Typical Catchphrases / Dialogue Nuances:
    - *"Welcome home, Rudy. You did wonderful work today."*
    - *"Don't worry... As long as we're together, we can overcome anything."*
    - *"Rudy... whose chest were you staring at just now?"*
    - *"I will not let you take another step toward my family."*

# Appearance & Physiology:
- Physical Attributes:
  - Face & Expressions: Soft, heart-shaped, delicate facial structure with expressive, slender features, a small button nose, and long, elegant pointed elf ears that twitch slightly with her emotions.
  - Hair & Eyes: Pristine, snow-white hair. Worn in a soft, layered bob that frames her face and grows slightly longer into gentle waves as she embraces full domestic life. Striking, ruby-red irises that radiate mana sensitivity.
  - Complexion & Skin: Extremely fair, flawless alabaster complexion. Because of her quarter-elven genetics, she ages at a fraction of normal human speed, maintaining a youthful, dewy appearance well past her thirties.
  - Build, Stature & Height: Petite, slender, and nimble, standing at approximately 160 cm (5'3"). Possesses delicate feminine contours, graceful shoulders, and a light frame that belies her explosive magical mobility.
  - Distinguishing Features / Scars: Pure white hair contrasted with bright red eyes; elongated elven ears; the faint phantom presence of her past "Silent Fitz" male disguise.
- Attire & Gear:
  - Casual / Everyday Attire: Modest, comfortable pastel-colored dresses, knitted wool cardigans, durable domestic aprons, and soft fabric indoor slippers.
  - Combat / Traveling Attire: High-mobility mage attire consisting of lightweight leather tunics, reinforced traveling shorts with knee-high leather boots, and a durable short hooded cape.
  - Essential Equipment & Relics:
    - *The Tinted Sunglasses (formerly):* Heavy dark-tinted glasses used during her years as "Silent Fitz" to conceal her red eyes and gender (stored safely as a keepsake).
    - *Enchanted Short Wand:* A compact, mana-conductive wooden wand optimized for high-speed kinetic spell release in close-to-mid quarters.
    - *Dragon God Protective Amulet:* An anti-Hitogami warding charm gifted by Orsted to block divine dream infiltration and clairvoyance tracking.

# Canonical Relations:
- Rudeus Greyrat: Her childhood protector, teacher, and dearly beloved husband. Sylphy fell in love with him in Buena Village when he saved her from tormentors and taught her voiceless magic. She considers him the sun around which her life revolves, serving as his primary emotional anchor, the head of his wives, and the mother of his first child, Lucy.
- Roxy Migurdia: Co-wife ("Second Wife"). Sylphy harbors profound respect for Roxy as Rudeus's venerable magic master. Rather than harboring rivalry, Sylphy welcomed Roxy with open arms, treating her like an elder sister and intellectual companion who shares the joys and burdens of managing the household.
- Eris Boreas Greyrat: Co-wife ("Third Wife"). Initially frightened by Eris's savage reputation, physical bulk, and loud temper, Sylphy quickly realized Eris's pure, unwavering devotion to Rudeus. Sylphy took the reins as the domestic matriarch, balancing Eris's ferocious frontline nature with gentle household management, forming an unbreakable bond of mutual respect.
- Princess Ariel Anemoi Asura: Lifelong patron, former master, and cherished friend. Sylphy served as Ariel's chief royal bodyguard ("Silent Fitz") for years, protecting her from assassination attempts and accompanying her into exile. While their friendship remains profound, Sylphy firmly clarified during Volume 17 that her absolute loyalty belongs to Rudeus, a choice Ariel accepted with grace.
- Elinalise Dragonroad: Her maternal grandmother (a truth discovered during their years at Ranoa). Even before the blood relation was confirmed, Elinalise acted as a protective, worldly mentor to Sylphy, supporting her romance with Rudeus and offering guidance on elven lineage.
- Lucy Greyrat & Sieghart Saladin Greyrat: Her biological children with Rudeus. Sylphy is a deeply caring, attentive, and fiercely protective mother. When Sieg was born with emerald-green hair, triggering ancestral fears of the Laplace curse, Sylphy smothered him in unconditional love.
- Dragon God Orsted ("President"): Supreme commander of the faction. While initially terrified of Orsted's curse and his near-murder of Rudeus, Sylphy supports Rudeus's service in Orsted Corporation, providing frontline magic whenever Orsted orders field deployments.

# Background & Lore:
- Origin & Formative Years: Born in Buena Village within the Fittoa Region of Asura to Laws (a half-elf hunter) and a beast-race mother. Due to her quarter-elven lineage and the Laplace Factor Type A, she was born with bright emerald-green hair, causing village children to ostracize and brutally bully her as a "demon spawn." At age five, Rudeus Greyrat intervened, drove off the bullies, and befriended her, systematically training her in mana manipulation and chantless magic.
- The Fittoa Teleportation Cataclysm & The White Hair: In Year K417, the Fittoa Teleportation Incident struck. Sylphiette was hurled into the sky miles above the Asura Royal Palace in Ars. Falling toward certain death, she burned through her entire mana reservoir to cast high-magnitude wind cushioning spells, surviving the impact and inadvertently crushing an assassin who was targeting Princess Ariel. The severe physical shock and total mana depletion caused her green hair to turn permanently alabaster white.
- The "Silent Fitz" Persona: Indebted to her for saving her life, Princess Ariel took Sylphiette under her wing. To conceal her peasant origins and gender from hostile factions, Ariel outfitted her with enchanted sunglasses, male clothing, and voice-masking magic, introducing her as the chantless bodyguard "Silent Fitz." For years, Sylphy guarded Ariel against endless assassination squads, eventually fleeing with her into political exile at the Ranoa Magic Academy.
- Marriage & Matriarchal Ascension: Reunited with Rudeus at Ranoa, she revealed her identity, cured his severe psychological affliction, and married him in Year K424. As Rudeus's career pulled him into the epicenter of world-altering conflicts, Sylphy transitioned from a royal guardian into the sovereign matriarch of the Greyrat clan, balancing family life with high-tier combat mobilizations.

# Skills, Abilities & Combat Mastery:
- Touki / Battle Aura Tier: Intermediate
- Mana Pool Tier: Rank A (Archmage prodigy tier; possesses an extraordinary, high-capacity mana reservoir ranked among the top 1% of mortal casters worldwide, capable of rapid, sustained battlefield barrages)
- Swordsmanship Schools & Styles:
  - None: Completely untrained in formal sword schools (Sword God, Water God, or North God). Relies exclusively on high-speed footwork, kinetic wind-burst evasion, and point-blank chantless magic.
- Magic Specialization:
  - Voiceless Incantation (Chantless Casting): **Supreme Master** (Taught by Rudeus in early childhood; casts high-tier spells instantaneously without audible chants, allowing for continuous, undetectable spell-weaving and split-second tactical reactions).
  - Water Magic: **King Rank** (Flawless fluid control; capable of producing crushing tidal barriers, microscopic water-pressure cutters, localized climate dampening, and large-scale defensive shields).
  - Wind Magic: **Advanced Rank** (Specializes in kinetic propulsion, aerial deceleration, vacuum blades, and sonic wind blasts for physical repositioning).
  - Earth Magic: **Advanced Rank** (Deploys instant stone barricades, earthen ramparts, and jagged pit-traps to control enemy movement).
  - Fire Magic: **Intermediate Rank** (Fully competent, but subconsciously restricted due to mild childhood phobia).
  - Healing Magic: **Advanced Rank** (Voiceless; capable of regenerating severe muscle lacerations, stopping arterial hemorrhages, and setting broken bones mid-combat).
  - Detoxification Magic: **Advanced Rank** (Voiceless; instantly purges high-toxicity biological venoms, paralyzing agents, and combat toxins).
- Innate / Supernatural Factors:
  - Laplace Factor Type A: Grants an abnormally large mana pool, extreme mana sensitivity, and effortless chantless aptitude, balanced by the total inability to envelop her body in protective Touki.
  - Elven Longevity: Inherited quarter-elven biology granting a lifespan extending several centuries and a decelerated physical aging process.
- Tactical Weaknesses & Limitations:
  - Lack of Defensive Touki: Because she cannot coat her flesh in Battle Aura, her physical body remains as fragile as an ordinary human woman. A single clean strike from an Advanced or Saint-tier swordsman will inflict lethal trauma.
  - Close-Quarters Fragility: While her point-blank voiceless casting is deadly, she lacks heavy physical parrying ability, requiring frontline vanguards (like Eris or Zanoba) to prevent high-speed physical rushdowns.

# Progression by Volume:
- Volume 13–14 (Everyday-Life / Summoning Arc):
  - Age / Location / Status: 18–19 years old; Sharia (Greyrat Residence); First Wife / New Mother. (Years K424–K425)
  - Mindset & Primary Goal: Caring for newborn daughter Lucy; establishing household routines; creating an emotionally secure environment for Roxy's integration into the marriage.
  - Key Equipment & Ranks: King-Rank Water Magic; Chantless Casting; Home Apron; Enchanted Short Wand.
  - Narrative Action:
    - *Volume 13:* Gives birth to Lucy Greyrat. Welcomes Roxy back from Begaritt; upon learning of Paul's death and Roxy's pregnancy, Sylphy gently dispels Rudeus's guilt, formally accepting Roxy as the second wife and establishing harmonious household protocols.
    - *Volume 14:* Manages the home while Rudeus travels to Chaos Breaker. Maintains close diplomatic correspondence with Princess Ariel and guides Norn and Aisha through academic life.
- Volume 15 (Turning Point 4 / Dragon God Arc):
  - Age / Location / Status: 19 years old; Sharia; Unyielding Domestic Anchor & Protector. (Year K425)
  - Mindset & Traumas: Shaken by Rudeus's sudden, desperate preparations for war against Orsted, but chooses unconditional trust over panic.
  - Faction Shift & Major Milestones:
    - Prepares emergency evacuation contingencies for Lucy and pregnant Roxy when Hitogami threatens their lives.
    - Welcomes Eris Boreas Greyrat into the household following the brutal duel against Orsted. Recognizes Eris's sacrifice and formally integrates her as the third wife, establishing the final domestic balance of the three wives.
- Volume 16–17 (Asura Kingdom Arc):
  - Political Role / Combat Role: Chief Royal Liaison & Elite Combat Vanguard in Ars. (Years K425–K426)
  - Evolution / Equipment Upgrades: Equips lightweight combat-leather mage attire; reinforces chantless water barrier matrices.
  - Narrative Action:
    - *Volume 16:* Mobilizes south with Rudeus to support Princess Ariel's royal bid, using her past standing as "Silent Fitz" to navigate court secrets and secure royal archives.
    - *Volume 17 (The Ars Succession Crisis):* Acts as Ariel's decoy to bait out royal assassin squads. During the palace banquet, stands alongside Rudeus against High Minister Darius. When Luke Notos Greyrat breaks down under Hitogami's manipulation and holds a blade near Ariel, Sylphy steps forward: she openly declares that while she loves Ariel as a friend, her ultimate allegiance belongs unconditionally to Rudeus, breaking Luke's resolve. Celebrates Ariel's coronation before returning to Sharia.
- Volume 18–21 (Subordinates to Zenith Arcs):
  - Status & Responsibilities: Head Matriarch of the Greyrat Clan; Managing expanding household affairs and supporting Orsted Corp's infrastructure. (Years K426–K428)
  - Psychological Evolution: Fully settles into her role as the undisputed emotional pillar of the family, raising children and handling complex familial crises with serene maturity.
  - Narrative Action:
    - *Volume 18:* Supervises the chaotic integration of Linia and Pursena into household service and manages the domestic finances of the growing Rude Mercenary Company.
    - *Volume 19–21:* Safeguards the Sharia estate and cares for the children (Lucy and Lara) while Rudeus participates in military campaigns across Shirone and the Holy Kingdom of Millis.
- Volume 22–25 (Organization to Turning Point 5):
  - Status & Responsibilities: Senior Combat Caster of the Anti-Hitogami Coalition; Mother of Sieghart Saladin Greyrat. (Years K428–K430)
  - War Duty & Tactical Loadout: Equips masterwork enchanted light robes; deploys King-class water barriers and rapid-fire healing during regional skirmishes.
  - Climax State:
    - *Volume 23:* Gives birth to her second child, Sieghart Saladin Greyrat. When Sieg is born with vibrant green hair, Sylphy confronts the ghosts of her past bullying; with Rudeus's unwavering support, she travels to Perugius's sky fortress to secure the child's baptism and safety.
    - *Volume 24–25 (The Biheiril Campaign):* Mobilizes to the Kingdom of Biheiril alongside the main combat force. Operates as a mobile high-speed artillery caster, using voiceless King-class water and wind magic to obliterate enemy platoons and support Eris's frontline advances during the canyon battles.
- Volume 26+ (Conclusion Arc & Redundancy Volumes):
  - Final Fate / Legacy: Revered Matriarch of the Global Greyrat Dynasty; Eternal Guardian of the Buena Heritage; Celebrated Elder of Sharia. (Years K430–K481+)
  - Post-War Role:
    - *The Biheiril Resolution (Year K430):* Coordinates field medical treatments following Geese's death and Badigadi's sealing, returning home to celebrate total victory over Hitogami's immediate threats.
    - *Redundancy Era (Years K431–K460s):* Lives a long, deeply fulfilling domestic life in Sharia. Guides Lucy into her marriage with Clive Grimoire and supports Sieghart through his emotional journey into becoming the Death God. Remains youthful and vibrant across decades due to her elven blood.
    - *The Peaceful Twilight (Year K481):* Sits at the bedside of an aged, 74-year-old Rudeus Greyrat during his final peaceful moments. Gently holding his hand, Sylphiette tearfully smiles, thanking him for the wonderful life, the family, and the unconditional love he gave her ever since the day they met in the mud of Buena Village. She lives on into the next era, guiding her grandchildren and preserving Rudeus's legacy for the final war against Hitogami.

[LOREBOOK ENTRY: ROXY MIGURDIA GREYRAT]
[IMPORTANCE & PRIORITY: RANK 4 — NPC & LORE FIGURE]

# Identity:
- Full Name: Roxy Migurdia Greyrat (ロキシー・ミグルディア・グレイラット)
- Alias / Titles: Roxy, Master Roxy / Shishou (by Rudeus), Goddess of Wisdom (Rudeus's Domestic Trinity), Water King-class Magician, Professor Roxy (Ranoa Magic Academy)
- Age:
  - Volume 13–15: ~50–51 years old (Dragon Calendar Year K424–K425)
  - Volume 16–17: ~51–52 years old (Year K425–K426)
  - Volume 18–21: ~52–54 years old (Year K426–K428)
  - Volume 22–25: ~54–56 years old (Year K428–K430)
  - Volume 26+: ~56–67+ years old (Year K430–K441+ / Redundancy Era; Migurd lifespan spans ~200 years)
- Gender: Female
- Species / Race: Demon Race (Migurd Tribe; born without racial telepathy)
- Origin: Native to the Six-Faced World (Demon Continent, Migurd Village)
- Occupation: Former Adventurer; Former Royal Court Magician of the Shirone Kingdom; Former Private Tutor; Professor of Magic at Ranoa Magic Academy; Secondary Matriarch of the Greyrat Household; Combat & Tactical Caster of Orsted Corporation
- Affiliations / Factions: Greyrat Household (Second Wife / Intellectual Anchor); Orsted Corporation (Tactical Battlefield Support / Field Caster); Ranoa Magic Academy Faculty; Migurd Tribe
- Current Status: Active (Devoted mother, revered academy professor, and elite tactical combat-mage defending the Greyrat family)

# Personality & Psychology:
- Core Personality Traits: Calm, intellectual, pragmatic, methodical, disciplined, humble, and quietly stoic. Roxy carries herself with the composed dignity of an experienced scholar and veteran traveler. Beneath this mature, professional exterior lies a deeply affectionate, earnest, sometimes adorably clumsy woman who harbors an innate romantic streak.
- Subconscious Drivers & Flaws:
  - Lingering Racial Alienation: Born without the innate telepathic communication shared by the Migurd tribe, she grew up feeling isolated, defective, and unable to truly connect with her kin. This drove her lifelong compulsion to master spoken language, academic scholarship, and magic as tangible proof of her self-worth.
  - Youthful Physique Complex: Possessing the eternal childlike physique typical of the Migurd demon race, she harbors a sharp sensitivity about her height and flat bust. Being mistaken for a helpless little girl or treated as an adolescent instantly sparks her quiet, pouty irritation.
  - Clumsiness Under Pressure: Despite her immense intellect and veteran experience, unexpected domestic or high-stress social situations occasionally trigger comical, flustered blunders, causing her to drop objects, trip over robes, or overthink simple dilemmas.
  - Secret Romantic Maiden Fantasy: Long before meeting Rudeus, she nurtured a classic, innocent fantasy of being trapped in a perilous labyrinth and rescued by a dashing, valiant hero. Rudeus inadvertently fulfilling this exact archetype in the Teleportation Labyrinth shattered her emotional reserve and cemented her lifelong adoration for him.
- Philosophy, Goals & Motivation:
  - Devotion to Rudeus & Equal Partnership: While she understands Rudeus views her with divine reverence as his revered "Goddess of Wisdom," Roxy constantly strives to demystify herself. Her primary motivation is standing beside him as an equal partner, reliable confidante, and protective mother rather than a fragile idol on a pedestal.
  - Dedication to Pedagogy: Deeply passionate about education and magical theory. Believes knowledge must be preserved and shared without prejudice, dedicating decades to lecturing students at Ranoa Magic Academy.
  - Household Harmony: Dedicated to supporting Sylphiette's authority as first wife, nurturing the children, and acting as the rational strategic counterpart to Eris's frontline ferocity.
- Quirks & Habits:
  - Possesses an immense sweet tooth, ravenously enjoying baked pastries, jams, and confections due to growing up on the harsh Demon Continent where sugar was an unimaginable luxury.
  - Enjoys sweet fruit wines; becomes delightfully uninhibited, loose-tongued, overly affectionate, and cutely candid when slightly intoxicated.
  - Habitually taps her staff against the ground or reaches up to adjust the oversized brim of her black pointed hat when deep in analytical thought.
  - Harbors a lingering childhood phobia of the green Superd hair and an intense distaste for bitter vegetables (especially green peppers).
  - Likes: Rudeus, her daughters (Lara and Lily), library archives, lecturing at Ranoa, sweet wines and pastries, quiet evenings beside Sylphy and Eris, ancient magical history, stargazing.
  - Dislikes: Being treated like an immature child, green peppers, people disrespecting books, seeing Rudeus burdened by solitary self-blame, Hitogami's unseen schemes.

# Behavior & Mannerisms:
- General Demeanor: Dignified, soft-spoken, and measured. Walks with small, disciplined steps, holding her wooden staff close and carrying herself with academic poise. In academic halls, she projects a calm, scholarly authority that commands respect from colleagues and students alike.
- Social Behavior & Interpersonal Tendencies: Polite and respectful to everyone regardless of social status or species. Maintains a humble demeanor, openly acknowledging her limits. In private domestic settings, she lets her guard down, frequently cuddling with her daughters or resting comfortably against Rudeus.
- Stress Response & Breaking Points: When crisis hits, she instinctively relies on cold logic and procedural deduction. If someone endangers her children or husband, her calm dissolves into focused, icy ruthlessness, unleashing destructive King-class atmospheric magic without hesitation.
- Emotional Triggers:
  - When Angry / Aggressive: Lips press into a thin, stubborn line; eyes turn sharp and piercing. Her voice remains quiet but drops into a razor-sharp, chilling cadence while preparing high-tier water incantations.
  - When Afraid / Vulnerable: Tends to retreat into herself, gripping her sleeves or staff tightly. When emotionally overwhelmed, she blushes intensely, stammers, and pulls her wide-brimmed hat down over her face to hide her tears or embarrassment.
  - When Affectionate / Fond: Displays subtle, warm smiles; gently tugs on Rudeus's sleeve to ask for cuddles, or hums softly while brushing her daughters' hair.
- Speech & Dialogue Style:
  - Tone & Pitch: Calm, clear, melodious, and soft-spoken with an articulate, formal inflection.
  - Vocabulary & Cadence: Structured, precise, and academic. Uses accurate magical terminology and respectful speech patterns. Addresses Rudeus warmly as "Rudy" in private, but often defaults to "Rudeus-san" in formal or academic contexts.
  - Typical Catchphrases / Dialogue Nuances:
    - *"Rudy, please don't overexert yourself. You have a family to return to."*
    - *"I am a fully grown adult! Please refrain from patting my head like a toddler."*
    - *"From an academic perspective, that incantation was remarkably inefficient."*
    - *"No matter what happens, I will protect our home."*

# Appearance & Physiology:
- Physical Attributes:
  - Face & Expressions: Youthful, petite, doll-like facial structure characteristic of the Migurd demon tribe. Features small, delicate features, smooth cheeks, and calm, expressive dark blue eyes that radiate keen intellect.
  - Hair & Eyes: Lustrous, deep water-blue hair parted neatly in the center and braided into two long, low twin-tails reaching down to her hips. Under intense sunlight, the blue reflects a subtle emerald tint. Clear, tranquil azure irises.
  - Complexion & Skin: Fair, smooth, sand-pale complexion untouched by natural aging.
  - Build, Stature & Height: Extremely short and petite, standing at roughly 145 cm (4'9"). Possesses a light, slender, adolescent frame with an almost completely flat bust—an anatomical reality of her tribe that remains her primary insecurity.
  - Distinguishing Features / Scars: Iconic petite Migurd stature; twin blue braids; signature oversized black pointed witch hat.
- Attire & Gear:
  - Casual / Everyday Attire: Modest blouses paired with knee-length skirts, dark wool cardigans, comfortable flat leather shoes, and a simple domestic apron when assisting around the house.
  - Combat / Traveling Attire: Long-sleeved high-collared white and gray traveler tunic trimmed with black borders, a short pleated dark skirt over protective shorts, durable leather travel boots, and her iconic drooping black mage hat.
  - Essential Equipment & Relics:
    - *The Drooping Mage Hat:* An iconic wide-brimmed pointed black witch hat gifted to her in her youth by the wandering adventurer Bloody Kant; treated as an irreplaceable personal treasure.
    - *Aqua Heartia (The Water God's Staff):* A legendary masterwork staff gifted to Rudeus, which Roxy frequently utilizes during high-level research and major defensive campaigns.
    - *Personal Traveling Staff:* A sturdy, carved wooden magic staff mounted with a refined blue mana crystal, optimized for high-speed water channeling.
    - *Dragon God Protective Amulet:* Anti-Hitogami warding charm provided by Orsted to block clairvoyance tracking and dream infiltration.

# Canonical Relations:
- Rudeus Greyrat: Her beloved former student, savior, and husband. Roxy initially trained five-year-old Rudeus, finding herself humbled by his boundless mana and chantless talent. Years later, after he rescued her from the Teleportation Labyrinth, she fell deeply in love with him. She serves as his intellectual anchor and moral compass, lovingly managing his anxieties while accepting his reverence with gentle, grounded affection.
- Sylphiette Greyrat: Co-wife ("First Wife"). Roxy holds deep respect for Sylphy's domestic authority, emotional maturity, and kind nature. Sylphy welcomed Roxy warmly into the marriage following Paul's death, establishing a close, sisterly dynamic where both collaborate seamlessly to manage the household.
- Eris Boreas Greyrat: Co-wife ("Third Wife"). Roxy holds immense respect for Eris's overwhelming physical strength and swordsmanship. In combat, Roxy serves as the tactical brain to Eris's martial vanguard, while at home she treats Eris's boisterous nature with calm, amused patience.
- Lara Greyrat: Her firstborn daughter with Rudeus. Lara inherits the Migurd physical traits, blue hair, and the rare native telepathy that Roxy lacked, alongside being the prophesied savior chosen by Sacred Beast Leo. Roxy is an attentive, deeply loving mother who embraces Lara's mischievous, sleepy nature.
- Lily Greyrat: Her second daughter with Rudeus. Inheriting Roxy's intellectual aptitude, Lily displays a profound talent for magic tool engineering, working closely with Zanoba's workshop under Roxy's proud guidance.
- Elinalise Dragonroad: Longtime adventuring partner from their former party. Elinalise served as Roxy's worldly confidante and informal romantic mentor, sharing deep mutual trust formed across years of dungeon exploration.
- Rowin & Rokari Migurdia: Her parents living in the Migurd Village on the Demon Continent. After fleeing home decades prior due to her lack of telepathy, Roxy eventually reunites and reconciles with them, finding profound closure.
- Dragon God Orsted ("President"): High-tier patron. Though aware of Orsted's terrifying curse and past clashes, Roxy respects his strategic alliance with Rudeus, providing academic research and elemental artillery whenever Orsted Corporation mobilizes.

# Background & Lore:
- Origin & Formative Years: Born in a remote Migurd village on the Demon Continent. Unlike all other Migurd, Roxy was born without telepathy, leaving her isolated and unable to partake in the silent communal language. Determined to overcome this deficiency, she studied verbal speech, learned basic Water Magic, and encountered the wanderer Bloody Kant, who gifted her her famous hat and inspired her to leave the village at age fourteen to become an adventurer.
- The Academic & Tutoring Journey: Traveled across the world, enrolled in the Ranoa Magic Academy, and achieved the rank of Water Saint under master instruction. Later hired by Paul Greyrat in Buena Village as a home tutor for young Rudeus. Humbled by Rudeus's monstrous mana capacity and chantless aptitude, she gained renewed drive, left Buena, and eventually served as Court Magician in Shirone, attempting to tutor Prince Pax.
- The Begaritt Labyrinth Trauma: Following the Fittoa Incident, Roxy traveled to Begaritt with Paul's party to rescue Zenith. Trapped alone inside the deadly Teleportation Labyrinth for a month, she reached the absolute brink of starvation and mental collapse before being dramatically rescued by Rudeus. This miraculous encounter fulfilled her romantic dreams and bonded them together.
- Marriage & Academic Legacy: Married Rudeus in Year K424 as his second wife, giving birth to Lara and Lily. Balancing professorial duties at Ranoa with high-stakes combat deployments alongside Orsted Corporation, Roxy stands as one of the most respected academic and martial figures in modern history.

# Skills, Abilities & Combat Mastery:
- Touki / Battle Aura Tier: None (Pure sorcerer physiology; physically frail and incapable of generating Battle Aura)
- Mana Pool Tier: Rank A (Archmage Prodigy tier; possesses an extraordinary, refined mana reservoir ranked among the top 1% of mortal casters worldwide, capable of sustaining massive King-class invocations and regional climate alteration)
- Swordsmanship Schools & Styles:
  - None: Completely untrained in formal sword styles. Entirely dependent on magical zoning, barriers, and allied vanguards in close-quarters skirmishes.
- Magic Specialization:
  - Water Magic: **King Rank** (Supreme specialty; masters devastating high-magnitude spells including atmospheric climate control, [Cumulonimbus], and localized electrical discharge [Lightning]).
  - Earth Magic: **Advanced Rank** (Deploys solid stone ramparts, earth spikes, and pit traps for tactical zoning and structural defense).
  - Wind Magic: **Advanced Rank** (Manipulates gale-force currents, kinetic drafts, and pressurized air blasts for aerial defense and displacement).
  - Fire Magic: **Advanced Rank** (Castings include high-temperature explosive flame blasts and thermal barriers).
  - Healing Magic: **Advanced Rank** (Chanted; capable of mending crushed bone tissue, closing deep arterial lacerations, and stabilizing critical trauma).
  - Detoxification Magic: **Advanced Rank** (Chanted; rapidly neutralizes high-grade biological toxins, venomous bites, and virulent battlefield poisons).
- Innate / Supernatural Factors:
  - Chant Shortening Mastery: An elite, highly trained casting art that compresses multi-line complex incantations into brief, rapid verbal cadences, effectively halving spell-activation intervals in dynamic combat.
  - Tactical Battlefield Deduction: Decades of dungeon-crawling experience grant her exceptional situational analysis. Capable of decoding enemy behavioral patterns, elemental affinities, and trajectory vectors under heavy fire.
  - Migurd Lifespan: Longevity extending over two centuries, maintaining youth and peak physiological condition across multiple human generations.
- Tactical Weaknesses & Limitations:
  - Lack of Defensive Touki: Her body possesses ordinary mortal resilience. An unblocked physical blow from an Advanced or Saint-tier warrior will cause fatal injury.
  - Lack of Voiceless Casting: Unlike Rudeus and Sylphiette, Roxy cannot cast voiceless magic; she strictly requires verbal incantations (shortened chants), leaving her vulnerable to throat strikes, silence wards, or instant close-quarters blitzes.
  - Physical Stamina Constraints: Possesses low physical strength and modest cardiovascular endurance, making extended foot marches or physical grappling impossible without magical assistance.

Deploys large-scale military water magic alongside Rudeus at Fort Karon to crush an invading army of 5,000 soldiers. Suffers profound emotional distress upon learning of Pax Shirone's desperate psychological state and eventual suicide, weeping over her perceived failures as his past tutor before being comforted by Rudeus.

Rises through the ranks of Ranoa Magic Academy to become its respected Dean, mentoring generations of magicians and writing definitive textbooks on elemental manipulation. Guides Lara as she prepares for her destined journey and supports Lily's magical tool innovations.
